)
(!NoticeStrangeLook!
	(if (equals (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You notice a very strange look Hilmi's eyes.  Something strange and savage.])
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [%s notices a very strange look Hilmi's eyes.  Something strange and savage.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))

	(cond (ask [Aldir, step away from her] [<let her go to him>])

;EVENT 116;

(#KilledHilmi#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^HilmiKilledAldir^)
		(  (callscript [GiveXP] 2)
			(flagp ^PartyRep^ 10)
			(if (flag ^KnowsDark^)
				(journal 579)
				(journal 580)
			)
		)
		(  (saychar ^Aldir^ 300 300 [My sweet little Hilmi.  What did they do to you...])
			(saydescchar ^Aldir^ 300 300 [Aldir trails off into a sob.])
			(callscript [GiveXP] 4)
			(flagp ^PartyRep^ 20)
			(if (flag ^KnowsDark^)
				(journal 576)
				(journal 578)
			)
		)
	)
	(removejournal 574)
	(sflag ^HilmiSlain^ 1)
)

);KilledHilmi;

;EVENT 121;
;TrillisReset;

(#TrillisReset#
(!begin!

	;lowering stats gives ability to pickpocket more easily;
	(setchar ^Trillis^ ^INTELLIGENCE^ 18)
	(setchar ^Trillis^ ^DEXTERITY^ 18)
	(setchar ^Trillis^ ^SPEED^ 18)
	
	;erase passed out schedule;
	(schedule ^Trillis^)
	
	;set his schedule back to normal;
	;night;
	(schedule ^Trillis^ 2935 485 2937 495 22 9 0 0)
	
	;day;
	(schedule ^Trillis^ 2938 482 2939 483 10 21 0 0)
	
	(push 1)
	(end)

)

);TrillisReset;

;EVENT 124;
(#GenwenLetter#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^GenwenSealed^)
		(goto [Letter Sealed])
		(goto [Letter Opened])		
	)
)

(!Letter Sealed!
	(cond (ask [<open letter>] [<leave the letter sealed>])
		(  (saychar 0 300 300 [You break the seal and open the letter.  It's blank.])
			(sflag ^GenwenSealed^ 0)
			(journal 241)
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

(!Letter Opened!
	(cond (ask [<attempt to reseal the letter>] [<leave the letter as is>])
		(goto [Seal])
		(	(push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)
)

(!Seal!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^DEXTERITY^) 17)
		(goto [Fail])
		(goto [Fail])
		(if (equals (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^LEAD^)
			(	(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You spend a few minutes reworking the seal, and are able to reseal the letter.])
				(sflag ^GenwenSealed^ 1)
			)
			(	(saychar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) 300 300 (string [%s spends a few minutes reworking the seal, and is able to reseal the letter.] (getchar (getbest 	^DEXTERITY^) ^Name^ )))
				(sflag ^GenwenSealed^ 1)
			)
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

(!Fail!
	(if (equals (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [Despite your best attempts to reheat the seal, you are not able to reseal the letter.])
		(saychar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) 300 300 (string [Despite %s's best attempts the letter remains unsealed.] (getchar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
);GenwenLetter;


;EVENT 125;

(#WatcherTempleBomb#
(!begin!

	(fade 0)

	;teleport party;
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 730 2775)

	
	;clearactions;
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	
	;teleport Kalliope;
	(removechar ^Kalliope^)
	(schedule ^Kalliope^)
	(place ^Kalliope^ 727 2768)
	(schedule ^Kalliope^ 727 2768 728 2769 0 23 ^NE^ 1)

	
	;flags;
	(sflag ^KalliopeDrag^ 1)

	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^N^)
	(dofor 15
		(callscript [UpdateParty])
	)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)

	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Explosive Packet^)
		(cond (ask [<bomb the Temple>] [<leave the Temple standing>])
			(goto [BombTemple])
			()
		)
		()
	)
	(end)
);Leave;
(!BombTemple!

	(fade 0)
	
	(callscript [GiveXP] 1)
	(take ^PARTY^ ^Explosive Packet^)
	(sflag ^WatcherBomb^ 1)
	(sflag ^RebIVTempleExploded^ 1)
	(fade 1)
	

	(saydescchar 0 300 300 [As instructed you light the bomb and throw it onto the altar.  As you run from the Temple the bomb explodes.  It completely destroys the altar along with a good portion of the rest of the temple.])

	(moveto ^PARTY^ 732 2770)
	
	(place ^Kalliope^ 730 2754)
	(cameralookat ^Kalliope^)
	(place ^Kalliope^ 727 2768)

	(explosion 730 2754 50 70 00 00 100 100 100)
	(dofor 30
		(
			(drawscreen)
			(updateall)
		)
	)
			
	(place ^Rubble^ 730 2764)
	(place ^Rubble^ 731 2764)
	(place ^Rubble^ 730 2765)
	(place ^Rubble^ 731 2765)
		
	(place ^Broken Board^ 731 2766)
	
	(place ^Rubble^ 724 2747)
	(place ^Rubble^ 724 2748)
	(place ^Rubble^ 723 2747)
	(place ^Rubble^ 723 2748)
	(place ^Broken Board^ 722 2749)
	
	(end)
)

);WatcherTempleBomb;

;EVENT 128;

(#possessedDead#
(!begin!

	(endcombat)

	(cameralookat (lookstack))

	(saydescchar 0 100 100 [The creature's blood curdling scream causes everyone to writhe in pain.])

	(fade 0)

	;set party action points;
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXACTIONPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^MAXACTIONPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY3^ ^MAXACTIONPOINTS^))

	;Derrack;
	(setchar ^Derrack^ ^AICODE^ 0)
	(push (placeclear ^Possessed^ (getx ^Derrack^) (gety ^Derrack^)))
	(setchar (lookstack) ^NAME^ [Strange Creature])
	(clearactions ^Derrack^)
	(placeclear ^Derrack^ (getx ^Damrush^) (add (gety ^Damrush^) 1))
	(setchar ^Derrack^ ^HITPOINTS^ (getchar ^Derrack^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^Derrack^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ 0)
	(clearactions ^Derrack^)
	(update ^Derrack^)
	(pop)
	
	;Senzo;

	(clearactions ^Senzo^)
	(placeclear ^Senzo^ (add (getx ^Damrush^) 1) (gety ^Damrush^))
	(setchar ^Senzo^ ^HITPOINTS^ (getchar ^Senzo^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^Senzo^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ 0)
	(clearactions ^Senzo^)
	(update ^Senzo^)
	(pop)
	
	;Guidian;
	(setchar ^Guidian^ ^AICODE^ 0)
	(push (placeclear ^Possessed^ (getx ^Guidian^) (gety ^Guidian^)))
	(setchar (lookstack) ^NAME^ [Strange Creature])
	(clearactions ^Guidian^)
	(placeclear ^Guidian^ (getx ^Damrush^) (gety ^Damrush^))
	(setchar ^Guidian^ ^HITPOINTS^ (getchar ^Guidian^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^Guidian^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ 0)
	(clearactions ^Guidian^)
	(update ^Guidian^)
	(pop)

	;Estabon;
	(setchar ^Estabon^ ^AICODE^ 0)

	(setchar (lookstack) ^NAME^ [Strange Creature])
	(clearactions ^Estabon^)
	(placeclear ^Estabon^ (getx ^Tia^) (gety ^Tia^))
	(setchar ^Estabon^ ^HITPOINTS^ (getchar ^Estabon^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^Estabon^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ 0)

	;Lugram;

	(setchar (lookstack) ^NAME^ [Strange Creature])
	(clearactions ^Lugram^)
	(placeclear ^Lugram^ (getx ^Bazir^) (gety ^Bazir^))

	;Rangard;

	(setchar (lookstack) ^NAME^ [Strange Creature])

	(cameraangle 250)
	(cameralookat ^Ciwdwr^)

	(saydescchar 0 100 300 [Hideous monstrosities now stand in place of your companions at arms.])
	(saychar ^Ciwdwr^ 100 300 (string [%s, %s, %s... you are the Valley's only hope.  You cannot fall!] (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^NAME^) (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^NAME^) (getchar ^PARTY3^ ^NAME^)))
	(saychar ^Ciwdwr^ 100 300 [Goddess aid me!])

	(dofor 25
		(  (update ^Ciwdwr^)
			(update ^Ciwdwr^)
			(drawscreen)

;EVENT 130;

(#KalliopeDrugged#
(!begin!
	(closetalk)
	(saychar ^Kalliope^ 300 300 [Who would have believed I was so hungry?])
	(saydescchar ^Kalliope^ 300 300 [Kalliope takes a couple of bites and starts to look very sleepy.  Suddenly she slumps over and passes out.])
	(take ^PARTY^ ^Tampered Meal^)
	(sflag ^GaveKalMeal^ 1)
	(sflag ^KalEaten^ 1)
	(sflag ^KalliopeDrugged^ 1)

	;kalliope slumps over;
	(animate ^Kalliope^ 30)
	(dofor 10
		(	(update ^Kalliope^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	;1 - remove current schedule;
	(schedule ^Kalliope^)
	(clearactions ^Kalliope^)
	;2 - replace with passed out schedule the 3 at the end indicates he's laying down;
	(schedule ^Kalliope^ (getx ^Kalliope^) (gety ^Kalliope^) (add (getx ^Kalliope^) 1) (add (gety ^Kalliope^) 1) 0 23 0 3)
	(update ^Kalliope^)


	(saydescchar 0 300 300 [Kalliope is unconscious.])

	(if (flag ^RebIVBombTemple^)
		(cond (ask [<drag Kalliope from the Temple>] [<leave Kalliope in the Temple>])
			(goto [Drag]);1 of 2;
			(goto [Leave]);2 of 2;
		);cond;
		()
	)
	(end)
);begin;
(!Drag!

	(fade 0)

	;clearactions;
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	
	;teleport Kalliope;
	(place ^Kalliope^ 730 2768)
	
	;1 - remove current schedule;
	(schedule ^Kalliope^)
	(clearactions ^Kalliope^)
	;2 - replace with passed out schedule the 3 at the end indicates he's laying down;
	(schedule ^Kalliope^ 730 2768 731 2769 0 23 0 3)
	(update ^Kalliope^)

	;teleport party;
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 730 2775)

	;flags;
	(sflag ^KalliopeDrag^ 1)

	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^N^)
	(dofor 15
		(callscript [UpdateParty])
	)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)

	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Explosive Packet^)
		(cond (ask [<bomb the Temple>] [<leave the Temple standing>])
			(goto [BombTemple])
			()
		)
		()
	)
	(end)
);Drag;

(!Leave!

	(fade 0)

	;clearactions;
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	
	;teleport party;
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 730 2775)

	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^N^)
	(dofor 15
		(callscript [UpdateParty])
	)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)
	
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Explosive Packet^)
		(cond (ask [<bomb the Temple>] [<leave the Temple standing>])
			(goto [BombTemple])
			()
		)
		()
	)
	(end)
);Leave;
(!BombTemple!

	(fade 0)
	(take ^PARTY^ ^Explosive Packet^)
	(sflag ^RebIVTempleExploded^ 1)
	(fade 1)
		
	(if (flag ^KalliopeDrag^)
		(	(saydescchar 0 300 300 [As instructed you light the bomb and throw it onto the altar.  As you run from the Temple the bomb explodes.  It completely destroys the altar along with a good portion of the rest of the temple.])
			(saydescchar ^Kalliope^ 300 300 [Kalliope is too drugged to know what is going on.])
		)
		()
	)
	
	(push (place ^Topi^ 730 2754))
	(cameralookat (lookstack))
	(removechar (pop))
	
	(explosion 730 2754 50 70 00 00 100 100 100)
	(dofor 30
		(
			(drawscreen)
			(updateall)
		)
	)

	(place ^Rubble^ 730 2764)
	(place ^Rubble^ 731 2764)
	(place ^Rubble^ 730 2765)
	(place ^Rubble^ 731 2765)

	(place ^Broken Board^ 731 2766)

	(place ^Rubble^ 724 2747)
	(place ^Rubble^ 724 2748)
	(place ^Rubble^ 723 2747)
	(place ^Rubble^ 723 2748)
	(place ^Broken Board^ 722 2749)


	
	
	(saydescchar 0 300 300 [As instructed you light the bomb and throw it onto the altar.  As you run from the Temple the bomb explodes.  It completely destroys the altar along with a good portion of the rest of the temple.])
	(if (flag ^KalliopeDrag^)
		()
		(
		 (saydescchar ^Kalliope^ 300 300 [You hear the muffled screams of the Temple Priestess inside...])
		 (removechar ^Kalliope^)
		  )
	)
	(end)
)

;EVENT 132;

(#AslofHunt#
(!begin!

	;fade & advancetime;
	(fade 0)	
	
	;clearactions;
	(clearactions ^Aslof^)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	;place mayor in field and arm him with a bow;
	(moveto ^Aslof^ 1451 1881)
	(setchar ^Aslof^ ^Strength^ 15)
	(give ^Aslof^ ^Wooden Arrow^ 100)
	(equip ^Aslof^ ^Ironwood Shortbow^)
	(sflag ^AslofHunting^ 1)
	
	;place party;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ (sub (getx ^Aslof^) 2) (sub (gety ^Aslof^) 2))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ (sub (getx ^Aslof^) 2) (gety ^Aslof^))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ (sub (getx ^Aslof^) 2) (add (gety ^Aslof^) 2))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ (add (getx ^Aslof^) 2) (add (gety ^Aslof^) 2))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ (add (getx ^Aslof^) 2) (gety ^Aslof^))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ (add (getx ^Aslof^) 2) (sub (gety ^Aslof^) 2))
			()
		)
	)
	
	;place topi;
	
	(push (placeclear ^Topi^ 1451 1869))
	
	;face;
	(face ^Aslof^ ^N^)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^N^)

	(dofor 12
		(
			(update ^Aslof^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)
	(camerazoom 10)
	(cameralookat (lookstack))
	(fade 1)
	
	(saydescchar 0 300 300 [After several hours of tracking you have found a large topi buck.  Aslof waits for you to take the shot.])
	(saychar ^Aslof^ 300 300 [There's some topi!  Quick, shoot it.])
	
	(if (flag ^RebVRemoveMayor^)
		(cond (ask [<shoot the topi>] [<shoot Aslof>])
			(goto [KillTopi])
			(goto [KillAslof])
		)
		(  (ask [<shoot the topi>])
			(goto [KillTopi])
		)
	)
)

(!KillAslof!
	(saydescchar ^Aslof^ 300 300 [Aslof's scream of horror is lost in the woods.  The topi scampers away.])
	(cameralookat ^Aslof^)
	(damage ^Aslof^ 213)
	(move (lookstack) 1459 1861)
	(dofor 15
		(
			(updateall)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(fade 0)
	(removechar (pop))
	(sflag ^AslofDead^ 1)
	(flagm ^PartyRep^ 30)
	(journal 260)
	(fade 1)

	(end)
)
(!KillTopi!
	(damage (pop) 213)
	(dofor 15
			(
				(updateall)
				(drawscreen)
			)
	)
	(saychar ^Aslof^ 300 300 [Great shot!  I'd better head back though.])

	(fade 0)

	;place;
	(place ^Aslof^ 891 2809)
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ 888 2806)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ 889 2806)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ 890 2806)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ 891 2806)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ 892 2806)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ 893 2806)
		()
	)

	;face;
	(face ^Aslof^ ^N^)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^S^)

	(dofor 12
		(
			(update ^Aslof^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)

	(setchar ^Aslof^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^Aslof^ ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Aslof^)

	(cameralookat ^Aslof^)

	(saychar ^Aslof^ 300 300 [That was fun.  We should do it again sometime.])
	(end)
)

	(end)

;EVENT 136;
;ConstructXbow;
;draft done;

(#ConstructXbow#
(!begin!

	(fade 0)
	(take ^PARTY^ ^Component 1^)
	(take ^PARTY^ ^Component 2^)
	(take ^PARTY^ ^Component 3^)
	(give ^LEAD^ ^Makeshift Crossbow^)
	
	(clearactions ^Bargilof^)
	(clearactions ^Ciwdwr^)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	(place ^Bargilof^ 1103 2207)
	(place ^Ciwdwr^ 1108 2202)
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 1097 2202)
	
	(face ^Bargilof^ ^NW^)
	(face ^Ciwdwr^ ^W^)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^E^)
	
	(dofor 15
		(
			(update ^Bargilof^)
			(update ^Ciwdwr^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)
	
	(saydescchar 0 300 300 [The slotted oars and fishing twine fit together to form a makeshift crossbow.  The platinum rod and arrowhead form a bolt, which fits nicely onto the crossbow stock.  The weapon stands ready to fire.])
	(saychar ^Ciwdwr^ 300 300 [Bargilof, what's going on?])
	(saychar ^Bargilof^ 300 300 [Do it!])
	(clearactions ^LEAD^)
	(equip ^LEAD^ ^Makeshift Crossbow^)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 1)
	(dofor 15
		(  (update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(saychar 0 300 300 [Choose Target])
	(goto [Choose Target])
)

(!Choose Target!
	;gettarget-animals or mayor;	
	(if (gettarget 2 (getx ^LEAD^) (gety ^LEAD^) 30 [Choose your target])
		(	;top of stack now has a creature;
			(if (equals (lookstack) ^Bargilof^)
				(goto [BargilofShot])
				(if (equals (lookstack) ^Ciwdwr^)
					(goto [CiwdwrShot])
					(goto [Anyone else shot])
				)
			)
		)
		(goto [Choose Target]) ;force player to pick a valid target;
	)
)

(!BargilofShot!
	(clearactions ^LEAD^)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 13)
	(dofor 15
		(  (update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage (lookstack) 7) ;instant death;
	(saydescchar (lookstack) 300 300 [Bargilof looks down at the bolt in his chest])
	(saychar (lookstack) 300 300 [Wha...])
	(saydescchar (pop) 300 300 [It seems that the bolt's magic was created for a specific target, and it doesn't kill Bargilof.  Instead Bargilof begins to transform.])
	(setchar ^Ciwdwr^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(flagp ^PartyRep^ 10)	
	(sflag ^FailedInfiltrate^ 1)
	(sflag ^RebUnderground^ 1)
	(sflag ^RebVIDone^ 1)	
	(fade 0)
	(place ^Transformed Bargilof^ (getx ^Bargilof^) (gety ^Bargilof^))
	(removechar ^Bargilof^)
	(face ^Transformed Bargilof^ ^S^)
	(dofor 10
		(update ^Transformed Bargilof^)
	)
	(fade 1)
	(end)
)
(!CiwdwrShot!
	(clearactions ^LEAD^)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 13)
	(dofor 15
		(  (update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(saydescchar (lookstack) 300 300  [Ciwdwr locks her gaze on you as the bolt penetrates her body.  She stands there for a moment with a look of disappointment and sorrow before collapsing to the ground.])
	
	;Ciwdwr collapses dead;
	(playsound 21)
	(damage (lookstack) 98) ;instant death;

	(saydescchar ^Bargilof^ 300 300 [Bargilof looks at you in outrage.])
	(saychar ^Bargilof^ 300 300 [You fool!  You must die for your failure!])
	(saydescchar ^Bargilof^ 300 300 [The rage expressed in Bargilof turns into something else as he begins to transform.])
	(setchar ^Ciwdwr^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(flagp ^PartyRep^ 10)	
	(sflag ^FailedInfiltrate^ 1)
	(sflag ^RebUnderground^ 1)
	(sflag ^RebVIDone^ 1)	
	(fade 0)
	(place ^Transformed Bargilof^ (getx ^Bargilof^) (gety ^Bargilof^))
	(removechar ^Bargilof^)
	(face ^Transformed Bargilof^ ^S^)
	(dofor 10
		(update ^Transformed Bargilof^)
	)
	(fade 1)
	(end)
)

);ConstructXbow;

;EVENT 138;


(#RebelFight#
(!begin!
	(cond (ask [<run>] [<fight>])
		(saychar 0 300 300 [You race out the door only to meet more members of the Rebellion...])
		(saychar 0 300 300 [Outside you hear more members of the Rebellion coming.  You race to meet them head on...])
	)
	(fade 0)

	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(clearactions ^Gwyd^)
	(clearactions ^Caryn^)

	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(  (moveto ^PARTY1^ 1327 2415)
				(face ^PARTY1^ ^NW^)
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(  (moveto ^PARTY2^ 1327 2416)
				(face ^PARTY2^ ^W^)
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(  (moveto ^PARTY3^ 1327 2417)
				(face ^PARTY3^ ^SW^)
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(  (moveto ^PARTY4^ 1328 2415)
				(face ^PARTY4^ ^NE^)
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(  (moveto ^PARTY5^ 1328 2416)
				(face ^PARTY5^ ^E^)
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(  (moveto ^PARTY6^ 1328 2417)
				(face ^PARTY6^ ^SE^)
			)
			()
		)
	)

	(setchar ^Caryn^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Caryn^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(face ^Caryn^ ^N^)
	
	(setchar ^Gwyd^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Gwyd^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(face ^Gwyd^ ^S^)

	(place ^Gwyd^ (getx ^LEAD^) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 9))
	(place ^Caryn^ (getx ^LEAD^) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 9))

	(push (placeclear ^Rebellion Thug^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
	(push (placeclear ^Rebellion Thug^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
	(push (placeclear ^Possessed^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
	(push (placeclear ^Possessed^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
	
	;Possessed;
	(  (give (lookstack) ^Vial of Darkstrength^)
		(face (lookstack) ^SE^)
	)

	;Possessed;
	(  (give (lookstack 1) ^Vial of Darkstrength^)
		(face (lookstack 1) ^NW^)
	)
	
	( 	(face (lookstack 2) ^NE^)
		
		;reset;
		(setchar (lookstack 2) ^AICODE^ 1)
		(setchar (lookstack 2) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

		;unequip;
		(setchar (lookstack 2) ^CHEST^ 0)
		(setchar (lookstack 2) ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
		(setchar (lookstack 2) ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
		(equip (lookstack 2))

		;equip objects;
		(equip (lookstack 2) ^Leather Hauberk^)
		(equip (lookstack 2) ^Longsword^)
	);reb thug 2;
	( 	(face (lookstack) ^W^)
		
		;reset;
		(setchar (lookstack 3) ^AICODE^ 1)
		(setchar (lookstack 3) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

		;unequip;
		(setchar (lookstack 3) ^CHEST^ 0)
		(setchar (lookstack 3) ^HEAD^ 0)
		(setchar (lookstack 3) ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
		(setchar (lookstack 3) ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
		(equip (lookstack 3))

		;equip objects;
		(equip (lookstack 3) ^Chainmail Shirt^)
		(equip (lookstack 3) ^Chain Leggings^)
		(equip (lookstack 3) ^Chain Coif^)
		(equip (lookstack 3) ^Longsword^)
	);reb thug 1;

	(pop)
	(pop)
	(pop)
	(pop)
	
	(dofor 15
		(
			(updateall)
		)
	)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar ^Gwyd^ 300 300 [Die traitors!])

	(fade 0)
	(placeclear ^Major Possessed^ (getx ^Caryn^) (gety ^Caryn^))
	(removechar ^Caryn^)

	(placeclear ^Major Possessed^ (getx ^Gwyd^) (gety ^Gwyd^))
	(removechar ^Gwyd^)
	(fade 1)

	(sflag ^RebelEndFight^ 1)

	(end)
)
);RebelFight;


;EVENT 139;

(#PlaceCaldus#

		(  (saychar 0 300 300 [In his haste to attack you, Caryn dropped something...])

				(give ^PARTY^ ^Caldus Ore^)
				(place ^Caldus Ore^ 1337 2401)
			)
			(push 1)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
);PlaceCaldus;


;EVENT 140;

(#partyDieOverride#
(!begin!


	(cond (comp (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^HITPOINTS^) 1)
		(saydescchar 0 100 100 (string [%s has fallen, all is lost.] (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^NAME^)))
		()
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^HITPOINTS^) 1)
		(saydescchar 0 100 100 (string [%s has fallen, all is lost.] (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^NAME^)))
		()
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^PARTY3^ ^HITPOINTS^) 1)
		(saydescchar 0 100 100 (string [%s has fallen, all is lost.] (getchar ^PARTY3^ ^NAME^)))
		()
		()
	)


	(fade 0)

	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^DIEOVERRIDE^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^DIEOVERRIDE^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^DIEOVERRIDE^ 0)

	;REMOVE CREATURES;

	;red muties;
 	(dofor 2
		(removecreatures ^Red Mutie^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 20) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 20) (add (getx ^LEAD^) 20) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 20))
	)

	;lil muties;
 	(dofor 2
		(removecreatures ^Lil Mutie^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 20) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 20) (add (getx ^LEAD^) 20) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 20))
	)
	
	;possessed;
 	(dofor 4
		(removecreatures ^Possessed^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 20) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 20) (add (getx ^LEAD^) 20) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 20))
	)


	(endcombat)


	;RID OF FANCY EQUIPMENT;

	;PARTY1;
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^CHEST^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^LEGS^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^FEET^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^HEAD^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^AMMO^ 0)

	;PARTY2;

	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^CHEST^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^LEGS^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^FEET^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^HEAD^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^AMMO^ 0)

	;PARTY3;

	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^CHEST^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^LEGS^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^FEET^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^HEAD^ 0)
	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^AMMO^ 0)

	;RE-EQUIP PARTY;
	(equip ^PARTY1^)
	(equip ^PARTY2^)
	(equip ^PARTY3^)
	(equipall ^PARTY1^)
	(equipall ^PARTY2^)
	(equipall ^PARTY3^)

	;RE-VITALIZE PARTY;
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^HITPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXACTIONPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^WILLPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXWILLPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY1^ ^RESTPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXRESTPOINTS^))

	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^HITPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^ACTIONPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^MAXACTIONPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^WILLPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^MAXWILLPOINTS^))
	(setchar ^PARTY2^ ^RESTPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^MAXRESTPOINTS^))

	(setchar ^PARTY3^ ^HITPOINTS^ (getchar ^PARTY3^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^))

;EVENT 143;

(#MalpiaTeaParty#
(!begin!
	
	;place trolls and malpia;
	(placeclear ^Malpia^ 22 53)
	(push (placeclear ^Malpia's Troll^ 27 54))
	(push (placeclear ^Malpia's Troll^ 29 52))
	(push (placeclear ^Malpia's Troll^ 21 48))
	(push (placeclear ^Malpia's Troll^ 23 46))

	;face trolls and malpia;
	(face ^Malpia^ ^NE^)
	(face (lookstack) ^NW^)
	(face (lookstack 1) ^W^)
	(face (lookstack 2) ^SE^)
	(face (lookstack 3) ^S^)

	(dofor 15
		(
			(update ^Malpia^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(update (lookstack 1))
			(update (lookstack 2))
			(update (lookstack 3))
		)
	)
	
	(pop)
	(pop)
	(pop)
	(pop)

	(cameralookat ^Malpia^)
	
	(saychar ^Malpia^ 300 300 [More for the tea party?  Sit down, sit down.  Right next to Maximus.])

	(cond (ask [Sure] [No thanks])
		(
			(fade 0)
			(moveto ^PARTY^ 20 44)
			(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
			(callscript [FaceDirection] ^SE^)
			(dofor 15
				(callscript [UpdateParty])
			)
			(fade 1)
			(saydescchar ^Malpia^ 300 300 [Malpia takes a bib off of one of the cave creatures and hands it too you.  Seemingly enraged at the loss of its bib, the monster lunges at you.])
		)
		(saydescchar 0 300 300 [Seemingly enraged at your refusal of the invitation, the monster lunges at you.])
	)
	
	(saydescchar ^Malpia^ 300 300 [The little girl scrambles behind a rock.])
	(removechar ^Malpia^)
	
	(startcombat)

	(push 2)
	(end)
)

);MalpiaTeaParty;

;EVENT 144;

(#SeeHana#
(!begin!
	(saychar 0 300 300 [You see something, on the shore.  Before you have the chance to get a closer look whatever it was plunges into the water and disappears.])
	(if (and (flag ^AogianToldStory^) (flag ^HeardSylkieGirl^))
		(cond (ask [Hana?] [<leave>])
			(goto [Hana?])
			()
		)
		()
	)
	(if (flag ^AogianToldStory^)
		(cond (ask [Hana?] [<leave>])
			(goto [Hana?])
			()
		)
		()
	)
	(if (flag ^HeardSylkieGirl^)
		(cond (ask [Sylkie Girl?] [<leave>])
			(goto [Sylkie Girl?])
			()
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Sylkie Girl?!
	(saychar 0 300 300 [There is no response.])
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Hana?!
	(sflag ^PlaceHana^ 1)
	(addlocation [Hana] 1150 2312 0 9 259 199)
	(saychar ^Hana^ 300 300 [In the distance you can see a small head poking above the water.  A small girl cautiously climbs to the shore.])
	(place ^Hana^ 1150 2312)
	(schedule ^Hana^ 1150 2312 1151 2313 0 23 ^E^ 1)
	(addlocation ^Hana^ 1150 2312)
	(cameralookat ^Hana^)
	(talk ^Hana^)
	(push 1)
	(end)
)

);SeeHana;


;EVENT 145;
;TinemaKilled;

	(push (lookstack))
	(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
	(push (placeclear ^BanditC^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 5) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
	(push (lookstack))
	(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
	(push (placeclear ^BanditD^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 5) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
	(push (lookstack))
	(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
	
	(equip (pop) ^Red Hat^)
	(equip (pop) ^Red Hat^)
	(equip (pop) ^Red Hat^)
	(equip (pop) ^Red Hat^)

	(push 1)

	(end)

;EVENT 148;

(#UlaanAttacked#
(!begin!


	(sflag ^LatestLatestEvent^ (flag ^LatestEvent^))
	(sflag ^LatestEvent^ 148)

	(fade 0)

	(sflag ^UlaanFight^ 1)

	(placeclear ^Ulaan^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) (rand 7)) )
	(equip ^Ulaan^ ^Ironwood Dagger^)
	(setchar ^Ulaan^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	
	(fade 1)

	(cameralookat ^Ulaan^)

	(cond (rand 5)

		;1.  hoppers;
		(  (dofor 3
				(placeclear ^Hopper^ (sub (getx ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)))
			)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [The Hoppers are attacking the traveler.])
		)
		;2.  pennigers;
		(  (dofor 3
				(placeclear ^Penniger^ (sub (getx ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)))
			)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [The Pennigers are attacking the traveler.])
		)
		;3.  lupins;
		(  (dofor 3
				(placeclear ^Lupin^ (sub (getx ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)))
			)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [The Lupins are attacking the traveler.])
		)
		;4.  mantids;
		(  (dofor 3
				(placeclear ^Mantid^ (sub (getx ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)))
			)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [The Mantids are attacking the traveler.])
		)
		;5.  bandits;
		(  (push (placeclear ^BanditA^ (sub (getx ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^Ulaan^) (rand 5))))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
			(push (placeclear ^BanditB^ (sub (getx ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^Ulaan^) (rand 5))))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
			(push (placeclear ^BanditC^ (sub (getx ^Ulaan^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^Ulaan^) (rand 5))))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
			(saychar 0 300 300 [The bandits are attacking the traveler.])
		)
	)

)
);UlaanAttacked;

		(face (lookstack) ^S^)

		(setchar (lookstack) ^NAME^ [Guard])

		(setchar (lookstack) ^NAME^ [Guard])

		(setchar (pop) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

		(setchar (pop) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

		(setchar (lookstack) ^AICODE^ 1)
		(setchar (pop) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

		(setchar (lookstack) ^AICODE^ 1)
		(setchar (pop) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	)

	(addeventendcombat 151)

	(end)
)

);IronOreCaravan;

	(flagm ^PartyRep^ 50)

	(push 1)
	(end)
)

	(fade 0)
	(clearactions ^Arginir^)
	(place ^Arginir^ 950 2703)
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 950 2700)
	
	(face ^Arginir^ ^N^)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^S^)

			(update ^Arginir^)

;EVENT 154;
;CompleteTome;

(#CompleteTome#
(!begin!
	(if (or (flag ^SirianToldStory^) (flag ^KastorToldStory^) (flag ^AmalthiaToldStory^))
		(	(saydescchar 0 300 300 [This is the unfinished manuscript that Kastor and Sirian have been working on.])
			(cond (ask [<attempt to finish the manuscript>] [<leave the manuscript unfinished>])
				(goto [Finish])
				()
			)
		)
		(saydescchar 0 300 300 [This appears to be an unfinished manuscript, apparently detailing the stories of the People of the River.  According to the Introduction the authors are Sirian and Kastor.])
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Finish!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) ^LITERACY AND LORE^) 31)
		(if (equals (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) ^LEAD^)
			(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [After reading the manuscript it's clear to you that you don't know enough of the Valley's history to write a proper conclusion.])
			(saychar (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) 300 300 (string [After reading the manuscript it's clear that %s doesn't know enough of the Valley's history to write a proper conclusion.] (getchar (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) ^Name^ )))
		)
		(if (equals (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) ^LEAD^)
			(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [After reading the manuscript it's clear to you that you don't know enough of the Valley's history to write a proper conclusion.])
			(saychar (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) 300 300 (string [After reading the manuscript it's clear that %s doesn't know enough of the Valley's history to write a proper conclusion.] (getchar (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) ^Name^ )))
		)
		(	(if (equals (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) ^LEAD^)
				(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [After a quick read of what has been written, you are able to write a conclusion and complete the manuscript.])
				(saychar (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) 300 300 (string [After a quick read of what has been written, %s is able to write a conclusion and complete the manuscript.] (getchar (getbest ^LITERACY AND LORE^) ^Name^ )))
			)
			(callscript [GiveXP] 1)
			(take ^PARTY^ ^Incomplete Tome^)
			(give ^LEAD^ ^Completed Tome^)
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
);CompleteTome;

;EVENT 161;
;minefield placed throughout Mirilich's Caves;
(#FindMirilich#
(!begin!
	(if (or (flag ^KnowsMirilichRoute^) (flag ^TinemaThiefDone^))
		()
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^) 2)
			()
			(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 1)
				(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
			)
			(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^) 10)
				(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 1)
					(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
				)
				(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 2)
					(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
				)
				(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^) 20)
					(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 2)
						(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
					)
					(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 3)
						(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
					)
					(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^) 30)
						(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 3)
							(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
						)
						(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 4)
							(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
						)
						(	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 4)
							(goto [Found Mirilichsign])
						)
					)
				)
			)
		)
	)
	(push 1)
	(end)
)

(!Found Mirilichsign!
	(flagp ^FindMirilich^ 1)
	(if (equals (getbest ^NATURE^) ^LEAD^)
		(goto [Lead])
		(goto [Not lead])
	)
)

(!Lead!
	(cond (rand 4)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You can tell that someone has been walking around in here, and recently.])
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You think you hear a sound from up ahead.  Possibly someone trying to be sneaky.])
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [This areas has been lived in, and recently.])
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You definitely heard something just ahead.])
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

(!Not lead!
	(cond (rand 4)
		(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) 300 300 (string [%s can tell that someone has been walking around in here, and recently.] (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^Name^ )))
		(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) 300 300 (string [%s heard a sound from up ahead.  Possibly someone trying to be sneaky.] (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^Name^ )))
		(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) 300 300 (string [This areas has been lived in, and recently.] (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^Name^ )))
		(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) 300 300 (string [%s definitely heard something just ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

);FindMirilich;

			(sflag ^FindMirilich^ 0)

;EVENT 165;
;LuciusBarricade;

(#LuciusBarricade#
(!begin!
	(addlocation [Lucius's Mansion] 106 1009 0 2 236 326)
	(saychar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [You out there!  You are not welcome!  Begone!  I warn you, we are very well armed!])
	(cond (ask [<try to convince him to let you in>] [<attempt to force the door>] [<attempt to pick the lock>] [<leave>])
		(goto [convince])
		(goto [force])
		(goto [pick])
		(  (push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)
)
(!convince!
	(fade 0)
	(fade 1)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^CHARISMA^) 19)
		(saychar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [You think I'll fall that easily for your tricks?  Get out of my home!])
		(saychar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [You think I'll fall that easily for your tricks?  Get out of my home!])
		(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^SPEECH^) 60)
			(saychar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [You think I'll fall that easily for your tricks?  Get out of my home!])
			(saychar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [You think I'll fall that easily for your tricks?  Get out of my home!])
			(  (saydescchar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [After a moment, Lucius cautiously opens the door a crack.  You hear people scampering around in the next room.])
				;open door;
				(push 1)
				(end)
			)
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!force!
	(fade 0)
	(fade 1)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^STRENGTH^) 20)
		(goto [failedforce])
		(goto [failedforce])
		()
	)
	(if (equals (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You successfully force the door.])
		(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [%s successfully forces the door.] (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(sflag ^BrokeIn^ 1)

	;open door and remove event;
	(push 3)
	(end)
)
(!failedforce!
	(if (equals (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You fail to force the door.])
		(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [%s fails to force the door.](getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(saychar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [Come now weaklings.  You don't think I would make it that easy for you to get in here do you?])
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!pick!
	(fade 0)
	(fade 1)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 30)
		(goto [failedpick])
		(goto [failedpick])
		()
	)
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You successfully pick the door's locks.])
		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s successfully pick the door's locks.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
	)

	;open door and remove event;
	(sflag ^BrokeIn^ 1)
	(push 3)
	(end)
)
(!failedpick!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You fail to pick the door's locks.])
		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s fails to pick the door's locks.](getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(saychar ^Lucius^ 300 300 [Come now you fumbling buffoons.  You don't think I would make it that easy for you to get in here do you?])
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

);LuciusBarricade;


;EVENT 166;
;GatesofJerrock;

(#GatesofJerrock#
(!begin!
	(if (or (flag ^CarynRebellion^) (flag ^KilledZeph^))
		(goto [FinishedJerrock])
		()
	)
	(if (flag ^SnuckInJerrock^)
		(goto [SnuckIn])
		(if (flag ^InfiltrateMiners^)
			(goto [TalkedtoZeph])
			()
		)
	)
	(cond (comp (gethour) 2)
	   (goto [Night])
	   ()
	   ()
	)
	(cond (comp (gethour) 20)
	   ()
	   ()
	   (goto [Night])
	)
	(goto [FailedStealth])
)
(!SnuckIn!
	(if (flag ^InfiltrateMiners^)
		(goto [TalkedtoZeph])
		(goto [FailedStealth])
	)
)
(!TalkedtoZeph!

	(if (getchar ^LEAD^ ^SEX^)
		(saychar ^Kheiron^ 300 300 [Not so fast lady.  Only reason you're alive is because you got a job to do, and you're not leaving until it's done.])
		(saychar ^Kheiron^ 300 300 [Not so fast guy.  Only reason you're alive is because you got a job to do, and you're not leaving until it's done.])
	)

	(fade 0)

	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ 111 952)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ 112 952)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ 113 952)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ 111 951)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ 112 951)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ 113 951)
		()
	)

	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^E^)
	
	(dofor 10
		(callscript [UpdateParty])
	)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)

	(fade 1)
	
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!FinishedJerrock!
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(saychar 0 300 300 [The party agrees that it would not be wise to re-enter Jerrock.])

	(fade 0)

	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 141 956)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 141 957)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 141 958)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 142 956)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 142 957)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 142 958)
			()
		)
	)

	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^E^)
	(dofor 10
		(callscript [UpdateParty])
	)

	(fade 1)
	
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Night!
	(if (flag ^LearnedForgeDagger^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 (string [The guards appear to be dozing.  %s thinks this would be the best time to try to sneak in.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [The guards appear to be dozing, but %s does not think it is a good idea to sneak in.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(cond (ask [<attempt to sneak in>] [<move back from the gates before you are noticed>])
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 35)
			(goto [FailedStealth])
			(goto [FailedStealth])
			(cond (rand 3)
				(goto [FailedStealth])
				(goto [PassedStealth])
				(goto [PassedStealth])
			)
		)
		()
	)
	
	(fade 0)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ 141 956)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ 141 957)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ 141 958)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ 142 956)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ 142 957)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ 142 958)
		()
	)
	(fade 1)
	
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

(!FailedStealth!
	(if (flag ^SnuckInJerrock^)
		(saychar ^Kheiron^ 300 300 [Hey!  They're trying to sneak out!])
		(saychar ^Kheiron^ 300 300 [Hey!  They're trying to sneak in!])
	)

	;make guards nigh impervious to damage, will take only 1/20th of damage dealt;
	(setchar ^Didius^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	
	(setchar ^Didius^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	
	;make guards do massive damage;
	(setchar ^Didius^ ^STRENGTH^ 30)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^STRENGTH^ 30)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^STRENGTH^ 30)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^STRENGTH^ 30)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^STRENGTH^ 30)
	
	;make guards hit frequently;
	(setchar ^Didius^ ^DEXTERITY^ 25)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^DEXTERITY^ 25)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^DEXTERITY^ 25)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^DEXTERITY^ 25)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^DEXTERITY^ 25)

	(setchar ^Didius^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	
	(setchar ^Didius^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	
	;make guards move rapidly;
	(setchar ^Didius^ ^MOVEPOINTS^ 1)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^MOVEPOINTS^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^MOVEPOINTS^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^MOVEPOINTS^ 1)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^MOVEPOINTS^ 1)

	(setchar ^Didius^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Flena^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kadmos^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Otho^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	

	(push 1)
	(end)
)
(!PassedStealth!
	(fade 0)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ 124 926)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ 124 927)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ 124 928)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ 125 926)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ 125 927)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ 125 928)
		()
	)
	(callscript [GiveXP] 5)
	(sflag ^SnuckInJerrock^ 1)
	(sflag ^DisableTravel^ 1)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You sneak in and find yourself in a small shantytown.])

	(push 0)
	(end)
)

);GatesofJerrock;

	(end)

;EVENT 168;
;MinerMeeting;

(#MinerMeeting#
(!begin!
	(sflag ^MinerMeeting^ 1)

	(fade 0)

	;stop people from what they are doing;
	(
		(clearactions ^Argant^)
		(clearactions ^Lutian^)
		(clearactions ^Prali^)
		(clearactions ^Thrain^)
	)
	
	;face them in a circle toward each other like they are in a meeting;
	(
		(place ^Argant^ 61 917)
		(face ^Argant^ ^SE^)

		(place ^Lutian^ 61 920)
		(face ^Lutian^ ^E^)

		(place ^Prali^ 65 917)
		(face ^Prali^ ^SE^)

		(place ^Thrain^ 67 917)
		(face ^Thrain^ ^S^)
	)

	;place party and jeda side by side and move them toward the meeting;
	(place ^Jeda^ 66 924)
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 64 925)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 65 925)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 66 925)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 64 926)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 65 926)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 66 926)
			()
		)
	)

	(face ^Jeda^ ^SE^)
	(move ^Jeda^ 61 918)

	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(move ^PARTY1^ 65 920)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(move ^PARTY2^ 66 920)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(move ^PARTY3^ 67 920)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(move ^PARTY4^ 65 921)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(move ^PARTY5^ 66 921)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(move ^PARTY6^ 67 921)
			()
		)
	)
	
	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Argant^)
			(update ^Lutian^)
			(update ^Prali^)
			(update ^Thrain^)
		)
	)

	(camerazoom 10)
	(cameralookat ^Jeda^)

	(fade 1)

	(dofor 70
		(
			(update ^Jeda^)
			(cameralookat ^Jeda^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(dofor 70
		(
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^NW^)
	(dofor 10
		(
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(cameralookat ^Argant^)
	(saychar ^Argant^ 100 100 [So, our friends arrive.])

	(cameralookat ^Lutian^)
	(saychar ^Lutian^ 50 100 [Friends indeed!  We know nothing about these people.  You are young and foolish Jeda, and I fear we will all have to pay for your naivete with our lives.])

	(cameralookat ^Thrain^)
	(saychar ^Thrain^ 100 100 [Hush Lutian.  What's done is done.  We have no choice but to trust Jeda's judgement now.  So let's waste no more time bickering and move on to the business at hand.])

	(cameralookat ^Argant^)
	(saychar ^Argant^ 50 100 [Agreed.  Much to do and little time to prepare.  Our friends here may prove to be our only hope in helping us with must be done.])

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(if (flag ^InfiltrateMiners^)
		(cond (ask [What must be done?] [I assure you, we will do whatever we can to help] [We have no interest in helping you][We come on behalf of Zephyros, prepare to die])
			(  (cameralookat ^Jeda^)
				(saychar ^Jeda^ 100 100 [Zephyros must be destroyed.  We must rob his armory so that we can have a fair chance of defeating House Baern.])
			)
			(  (cameralookat ^Jeda^)
				(saychar ^Jeda^ 100 100 [I knew that I could trust you.  Zephyros must be destroyed.  We must rob his armory so that we can have a fair chance of defeating House Baern.])
			)
			(goto [MinersAttack])
			(goto [MinersAttack])
		);cond;
		(cond (ask [What must be done?] [I assure you, we will do whatever we can to help] [We have no interest in helping you])
			(  (cameralookat ^Jeda^)
				(saychar ^Jeda^ 100 100 [Zephyros must be destroyed.  We must rob his armory so that we can have a fair chance of defeating House Baern.])
			)
			(  (cameralookat ^Jeda^)
				(saychar ^Jeda^ 100 100 [I knew that I could trust you.  Zephyros must be destroyed.  We must rob his armory so that we can have a fair chance of defeating House Baern.])
			)
			(goto [MinersAttack])
		);cond;
	);if;	
	
	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(cond (ask [Isn't that a bit extreme?] [We will help you] [We will not help you])
		(  (cameralookat ^Argant^)
			(saychar ^Argant^ 100 100 [Quite so.  But only the most dire of circumstances have pushed us to this extreme.  Forceful actions must be taken soon or River Folk will have no choice but to live as slaves in Jerrock.  So, are you, or aren't you with us?])
			(  (cameralookat ^LEAD^)
				(cond (ask [We're with you] [We will not help you])
					(goto [HelpMiners])
					(goto [MinersAttack])
				)
			)
		)
		(goto [HelpMiners])
		(goto [MinersAttack])
	)
	(journal 286)
	(end)
);begin;
(!HelpMiners!
	(cameralookat ^Jeda^)
	(saychar ^Jeda^ 100 100 [See Lutian?  I was right.  We can trust them.])

	(cameralookat ^Lutian^)
	(saychar ^Lutian^ 100 150 [That remains to be seen youngin'.])

	(talk ^Argant^)
)
(!MinersAttack!
	(cameralookat ^Lutian^)
	(saychar ^Lutian^ 100 100 [I knew that we couldn't trust them!  Quick, we must kill them before they escape.])
	(equip ^Jeda^ ^Long Dagger^)
	(equip ^Argant^ ^Spear^)
	(equip ^Lutian^ ^Mace^)
	(equip ^Prali^ ^Battleaxe^)
	(equip ^Thrain^ ^Mace^)

	(setchar ^Jeda^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Argant^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Lutian^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Prali^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Thrain^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

	(setchar ^Jeda^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Argant^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Lutian^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Prali^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Thrain^ ^AICODE^ 1)

	;zeph will now let you know that you can't help him;
	(sflag ^KilledMiners^ 1)


	(end)
)

);MinerMeeting;


;EVENT 169;
;MeetSubei;

(#MeetSubei#
(!begin!

	(fade 0)
	
	(removechar ^Jereath^)

	;stop people from what they are doing;
	(
		(clearactions ^Subei^)
		(clearactions ^Jeda^)
		(clearactions ^Argant^)
		(clearactions ^Lutian^)
		(clearactions ^Prali^)
		(clearactions ^Thrain^)
	)

	;face everyone;
	(
		(removechar ^Subei^)
		(schedule ^Subei^)
		(place ^Subei^ 108 895)
		(schedule ^Subei^ 108 895 109 896 0 24 ^W^ 1)

		(removechar ^Argant^)
		(schedule ^Argant^)
		(place ^Argant^ 104 896)
		(schedule ^Argant^ 104 896 105 897 0 24 ^N^ 1)

		(removechar ^Lutian^)
		(schedule ^Lutian^)
		(place ^Lutian^ 105 893)
		(schedule ^Lutian^ 105 893 106 894 0 24 ^S^ 1)

		(removechar ^Prali^)
		(schedule ^Prali^)
		(place ^Prali^ 101 896)
		(schedule ^Prali^ 101 896 102 897 0 24 ^N^ 1)

		(removechar ^Thrain^)
		(schedule ^Thrain^)
		(place ^Thrain^ 102 892)
		(schedule ^Thrain^ 102 892 103 893 0 24 ^S^ 1)

		(removechar ^Jeda^)
		(schedule ^Jeda^)
		(place ^Jeda^ 107 894)
		(schedule ^Jeda^ 107 894 108 895 0 24 ^SW^ 1)

	)

	;place party and jeda;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 104 893)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 104 894)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 104 895)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 103 893)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 103 894)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 103 895)
			()
		)
	)
	
	(
		(face ^Subei^ ^W^)
		(face ^Jeda^ ^SW^)
		(face ^Thrain^ ^S^)
		(face ^Prali^ ^N^)
		(face ^Argant^ ^N^)
		(face ^Lutian^ ^S^)
		(callscript [FaceDirection] ^E^)
	)
	(camerazoom 10)
	(cameralookat ^Subei^)

	(fade 1)

	(dofor 20
		(
			(update ^Subei^)
			(update ^Argant^)
			(update ^Lutian^)

			(update ^Thrain^)

;EVENT 172;
;BaernCall;

(#BaernCall#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^NoSneak^)
		(  (push 1)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 25)
		(goto [GuardCall])
		(goto [GuardCall])
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 30)
			(cond (rand 2)
				(goto [GuardCall])
				()
			)
			(cond (rand 2)
				(goto [GuardCall])
				()
			)
			(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 40)
				(cond (rand 3)
					(goto [GuardCall])
					()
					()
				)
				(cond (rand 3)
					(goto [GuardCall])
					()
					()
				)
				()
			)
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!GuardCall!
	(fade 0)
	(sflag ^JerrockGuardCall^ 1)	
	(sflag ^NoSneak^ 1)	

	;place guards;
	(
		(clearactions ^Aias^)
		(clearactions ^Didius^)
		(clearactions ^Flena^)
		(clearactions ^Gaius^)
		(clearactions ^Kadmos^)
		(clearactions ^Kheiron^)
		(clearactions ^Manto^)
		(clearactions ^Ocala^)
		(clearactions ^Otho^)
		(clearactions ^Peleus^)
		(clearactions ^Teucer^)
		(clearactions ^Gorgias^)
		(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	)
	(
		(placeclear ^Aias^ (getx ^LEAD^) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 9))
		(placeclear ^Didius^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 5) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 7))
		(placeclear ^Flena^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 5))
		(placeclear ^Gaius^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 9) (gety ^LEAD^))
		(placeclear ^Kadmos^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5))
		(placeclear ^Kheiron^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 5) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 7))
		(placeclear ^Manto^ (getx ^LEAD^) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 9))
		(placeclear ^Ocala^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 5) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 7))
		(placeclear ^Otho^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5))
		(placeclear ^Peleus^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 9) (gety ^LEAD^))
		(placeclear ^Teucer^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 5))
		(placeclear ^Gorgias^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 5) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 7))
	)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(camerazoom 10)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar ^Kheiron^ 300 300 [I don't know how you got in here, but you won't be leaving in one piece.])

	;move guards;
	(
		(move ^Aias^ (getx ^LEAD^) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4))
		(move ^Didius^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 2) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 3))
		(move ^Flena^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 3) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 2))
		(move ^Gaius^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 4) (gety ^LEAD^))
		(move ^Kadmos^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 3) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 2))
		(move ^Kheiron^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 2) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 3))
		(move ^Manto^ (getx ^LEAD^) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 4))
		(move ^Ocala^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 3))
		(move ^Otho^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 3) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 2))
		(move ^Peleus^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 4) (gety ^LEAD^))
		(move ^Teucer^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 3) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 2))
		(move ^Gorgias^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 3))
	)

	;animate party;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(animate ^PARTY1^ 0)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(animate ^PARTY2^ 0)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(animate ^PARTY3^ 0)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(animate ^PARTY4^ 0)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(animate ^PARTY5^ 0)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(animate ^PARTY6^ 0)
			()
		)
	)
	
	(dofor 20
		(
			(update ^Aias^)
			(update ^Didius^)
			(update ^Flena^)
			(update ^Gaius^)
			(update ^Kadmos^)
			(update ^Kheiron^)
			(update ^Manto^)
			(update ^Ocala^)
			(update ^Otho^)
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update ^Teucer^)
			(update ^Gorgias^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	;place all guards except for kadmos;
	;otho and kheiron to their original spots;
	(event 173)
	(push 1)
	(end)
);GuardCall;

);BaernCall;


;EVENT 173;
;MeetZephyros;

(#MeetZephyros#
(!begin!

	(sflag ^DisableTravel^ 1)

	(fade 0)
	(
		(clearactions ^Kheiron^)
		(clearactions ^Zephyros^)
		(clearactions ^Rhesos^)
		(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	)
	(
		;zeph should not need to be placed in the real game;
		(place ^Zephyros^ 85 1018)
		(place ^Kheiron^ 87 1013)
		(place ^Rhesos^ 87 1017)
	)

	(
		(face ^Zephyros^ ^N^)
		(face ^Kheiron^ ^SW^)
		(face ^Rhesos^ ^NW^)
	)

	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Zephyros^)
			(update ^Kheiron^)
			(update ^Rhesos^)
		)
	)

	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 86 1015)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 85 1015)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 84 1015)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 86 1014)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 85 1014)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 84 1014)
			()
		)
	)

	(camerazoom 10)
	(cameralookat ^Zephyros^)
	;camerarotate;

	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^S^)
	(dofor 10
		(callscript [UpdateParty])
	)
		
	(fade 1)

	(talk ^Zephyros^)
	(end)
)

);MeetZephyros;


;EVENT 174;
;ZephKillsParty;

(#ZephKillsParty#
(!begin!

	(equip ^Zephyros^ ^Longsword of the Flame^)
	(equip ^Rhesos^ ^Long Dagger of the Flame^)

	;make invincible, i.e. take only 1/20th of damage dealt;
	(setchar ^Zephyros^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	(setchar ^Zephyros^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)
	(setchar ^Rhesos^ ^RESISTNORMAL^ 5)
	(setchar ^Rhesos^ ^RESISTFIRE^ 5)

	(setchar ^Zephyros^ ^STRENGTH^ 25)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^STRENGTH^ 25)
	(setchar ^Rhesos^ ^STRENGTH^ 25)

	(setchar ^Zephyros^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Rhesos^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

	(setchar ^Zephyros^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Kheiron^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Rhesos^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(end)
)

);ZephKillsParty;


;EVENT 175;
;PeleusFight;

(#PeleusFight#
(!begin!

	(fade 0)

	;clear actions;
	(clearactions ^Peleus^)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	;place people;
	(placeclear ^Peleus^ (add (getx ^Peleus^) 11) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 3))
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ (add (getx ^Peleus^) 5) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 3))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ (add (getx ^Peleus^) 4) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ (add (getx ^Peleus^) 3) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 5))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ (add (getx ^Peleus^) 5) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ (add (getx ^Peleus^) 4) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 5))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ (add (getx ^Peleus^) 3) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 6))
		()
	)
	(moveto (lookstack) (sub (getx ^Peleus^) 1) (add (gety ^Peleus^) 1))

	;face people;
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(face ^PARTY1^ ^W^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(face ^PARTY1^ ^W^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(face ^PARTY1^ ^W^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(face ^PARTY1^ ^W^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(face ^PARTY1^ ^W^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(face ^PARTY1^ ^W^)
		()
	)

	(face ^Peleus^ ^SW^)
	(face (lookstack) ^NE^)

	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)

	;unequip fighters;
	(setchar ^Peleus^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^Peleus^ ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Peleus^)
	(if (getchar (lookstack) ^RIGHTHAND^)
		(give (lookstack) (getchar (lookstack) ^RIGHTHAND^))
		()
	)
	(if (getchar (lookstack) ^LEFTHAND^)
			(give (lookstack) (getchar (lookstack) ^LEFTHAND^))
			()
	)
	
	
	(setchar (lookstack) ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar (lookstack) ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(equip (lookstack))

	(camerazoom 10)
	;camera rotate;
	(fade 1)

	(clearactions ^Peleus^)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	(cameralookat ^Peleus^)
	(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Take your best shot whelp.])

	(cond (comp (getchar (lookstack) ^UNARMED^) 30)
		(cond (comp (getchar (lookstack) ^UNARMED^) 10)
			(cond (comp (getchar (lookstack) ^STRENGTH^) 18)
				(goto [BadBeating])
				(goto [BadBeating])
				(goto [Beating])
			)
			(cond (comp (getchar (lookstack) ^STRENGTH^) 18)
				(goto [BadBeating])
				(goto [BadBeating])
				(goto [Beating])
			)
			(goto [Beating])
		)
		(goto [Beating])
		(cond (comp (getchar (lookstack) ^STRENGTH^) 19)
			(goto [WonFight])
			(goto [WonFight])
			(goto [KnockOut])
		)
	)
);begin;
(!BadBeating!

	;player hits peleus but takes no damage;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(dofor 25
		(
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(saychar (lookstack) 100 100 (string [Peleus pauses and looks at %s in disgust.] (getchar (lookstack) ^NAME^)))
	(if (getchar (lookstack) ^SEX^)
		(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [You must be kidding me.  Is that all you got woman?  My turn.])
		(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [You must be kidding me.  Is that all you got little boy?  My turn.])
	)		

	(goto [FinishOff])

);BadBeating;
(!Beating!
	;player hits and damages peleus;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(animate ^Peleus^ 17)
	(dofor 15
		(
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Peleus^ (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 8) (lookstack))


	(if (getchar (lookstack) ^SEX^)
		(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [That's the best you got woman?  My turn.])
		(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [That's the best you got little boy?  My turn.])
	)		

	(goto [FinishOff])

);Beating;
(!FinishOff!
	;peleus hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Peleus^ 15)
	(animate (lookstack) 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage (lookstack) (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 5) ^Peleus^)

	;peleus hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Peleus^ 15)
	(animate (lookstack) 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage (lookstack) (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 4) ^Peleus^)

	;peleus hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Peleus^ 15)
	(animate (lookstack) 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage (lookstack) (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 3) ^Peleus^)

	;peleus hits and damages player;
	(clearactions ^Peleus^)
	(clearactions (lookstack))
	(animate ^Peleus^ 15)
	(animate (lookstack) 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage (lookstack) (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 3) ^Peleus^)

	(saydescchar (lookstack) 100 100 (string [%s cringes from Peleus's blows.  It's clear this fight is over.] (getchar (lookstack) ^NAME^)))
	(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Just as I thought.  Now, unless another of you wants a beating, scamper off.])
	(sflag ^TalkedtoPeleus^ 1)

	(fade 0)
	;place peleus back beating gyrraent;
	(clearactions ^Peleus^)
	(place ^Peleus^ (sub (getx ^Peleus^) 11) (sub (gety ^Peleus^) 3))
	(equip ^Peleus^ ^Greatsword^)

	(fade 1)

	(pop)
	(end)
);FinishOff;
(!WonFight!

	;player hits and damages peleus;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(animate ^Peleus^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Peleus^ (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 4) (lookstack))


	(saydescchar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Peleus rubs his jaw.])
	(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Lucky shot.  My turn.])

	;peleus hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Peleus^ 15)
	(animate (lookstack) 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(dofor 14
		(
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage (lookstack) (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 5) ^Peleus^)

	(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [What the?!?])

	;player hits and damages peleus;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(animate ^Peleus^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Peleus^ (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 4) (lookstack))

	;player hits and damages peleus;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(animate ^Peleus^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Peleus^ (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 3) (lookstack))

	;player hits and damages peleus;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(animate ^Peleus^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Peleus^ (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 3) (lookstack))

	;player hits and damages peleus;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(animate ^Peleus^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Peleus^ (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 2) (lookstack))


	(saydescchar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Peleus staggers back.])
	(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Enough, enough.  I'll leave him alone, alright?  Just don't tell anyone about this.])
	(if (flag ^MinerFriend^)
		()
		(  (callscript [GiveXP] 4)
			(sflag ^MinerFriend^ 1)
		)
	)
	(journal 816)
	(sflag ^PeleusHumiliated^ 1)
	(fade 0)
	(removechar ^Peleus^)
	(fade 1)
	(pop)
	(end)
);WonFight;
(!KnockOut!

	;player hits and damages peleus;
	(animate (lookstack) 15)
	(animate ^Peleus^ 17)
	(dofor 15
		(
			(update (lookstack))
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Peleus^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Peleus^ (div (getchar (lookstack) ^HITPOINTS^) 2) (lookstack))

	(saydescchar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Peleus staggers back in disbelief.  It's clear he's lost his interest in fighting.])
	(saychar ^Peleus^ 100 100 [Enough, enough.  I'll leave him alone alright?  Just don't tell anyone about this.])
	(if (flag ^MinerFriend^)
		()
		(  (callscript [GiveXP] 4)
			(sflag ^MinerFriend^ 1)
		)
	)
	(journal 816)
	(sflag ^PeleusHumiliated^ 1)
	(fade 0)
	(removechar ^Peleus^)
	(fade 1)
	(pop)
	(end)
);Knockout;

);PeleusFight;

;EVENT 188;
 
(#CarynBox#
(!begin!
	(cond (ask [<open the box>] [<leave the box alone>])
		(
			(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
			(saychar 0 -1 -1 [You hear something click.]) ;centers automatically;
			(explosion (getx ^LEAD^) (gety ^LEAD^) 50 70 150 250 100 100 100)
			(dofor 30
				(
					(drawscreen)
					(updateall)
				)
			)
			(fade 0)
			(endgame)
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

;EVENT 189;
;PeleusDead;

(#PeleusDead#

;EVENT 199;
;KilledAJerrCD;

(#KilledAJerrCD#
(!begin!
	(push 0)
	
	(flagp ^KilledAJerrCD^ 1)

	;after you have killed the last 2 cavern dwellers;
	;total killed is now 15;
	(if (equals (flag ^KilledAJerrCD^) 5)
		(goto [ClearedCavern])
		()
	)

	;after you have killed the fourth group of 3 cavern dwellers;
	;total killed is now 13;
	(if (equals (flag ^KilledAJerrCD^) 4)
		(goto [PlaceLastGroup])
		()
	)

	;after you have killed the third group of 4 cavern dwellers;
	;total killed is now 10;
	(if (equals (flag ^KilledAJerrCD^) 3)
		(goto [PlaceFourthGroup])
		()
	)

	;after you have killed the second group of 3 cavern dwellers;
	;total killed is now 6;
	(if (equals (flag ^KilledAJerrCD^) 2)
		(goto [PlaceThirdGroup])
		()
	)

	;after you have killed the first group of 3 cavern dwellers;
	;totatl killed is now 3;
	(if (equals (flag ^KilledAJerrCD^) 1)
		(goto [PlaceSecondGroup])
		()
	)

	(end)
)
(!PlaceSecondGroup!

	(if (getchar ^Llerwm^ ^HITPOINTS^)
		()
		(  (fade 0)
			(endgame [You are lost deep within the cavern system with no guide.])
		)
	)

	(saychar ^Llerwm^ 300 300 [Ha!  The blood feels good on my hands.  But there's much more fun to be had.  Follow me!])
	(saychar 0 300 300 [You follow Llerwm further into the cavern.])

	(fade 0)


	;THE PROBLEM STARTS HERE;

	;clearactions;
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(clearactions ^Llerwm^)

	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ 30 95)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ 34 94)
		()
	)

		(moveto ^PARTY3^ 37 94)
		()
	)

		(moveto ^PARTY4^ 31 92)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ 34 92)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ 37 92)
		()
	)

	(push (placeclear ^Angry Digger^ 37 104))
	(push (placeclear ^Angry Digger^ 41 103))

	(face ^Llerwm^ ^S^)

	(face (lookstack 2) ^NW^)

	(pop)

	(pop)

(#ZephFetchesSubei#
(!begin!
	(fade 0)


	;clearactions;
	(

		(saychar ^Subei^ 300 300 [But... you...  after all you've done.  How could you?  I was a fool to trust one of your kind.    My foolishness and your treachery will cost many of my people their lives.  Know this serpent, my spirit shall not rest until you pay for each one tenfold!!!])
		(saychar ^Subei^ 300 300 [But... you...  after all you've done.  How could you?  I was a fool to trust one of your kind.    My foolishness and your treachery will cost many of my people their lives.  Know this serpent, my spirit shall not rest until you pay for each one tenfold!!!])
		(saychar ^Subei^ 300 300 [You!  How could you?  The blood of your own people is on your hands, and its taint shall never be washed clean.])
	)
	(saydescchar ^Zephyros^ 300 300 [Zephyros laughs uproariously.])
	(saychar ^Zephyros^ 300 300 [The boy has spirit.  You have to admire that.  But entertaining as he is, I think his time has come.])

;EVENT 202;
;BreakBoard;

(#BreakBoard#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^OpenedJerrockFence^)
		(goto [AlreadyBrokeIn])
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 13)
		()
		()
		(goto [SawLooseBoard])
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!SawLooseBoard!
	(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [%s notices a loose panel.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
	(cond (ask [<attempt to break the panel>] [<leave the panel alone>])
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^STRENGTH^) 20)
			(goto [FailedBreak])
			(goto [FailedBreak])
			(goto [SucceededBreak])
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!FailedBreak!
	(fade 0)
	(fade 1)
	(if (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^SEX^)
		(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [%s bashes the panel as hard as she can.  It does not budge.] (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^NAME^)))
		(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [%s bashes the panel as hard as he can.  It does not budge.] (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!SucceededBreak!
	(fade 0)
	(sflag ^OpenedJerrockFence^ 1)
	(callscript [GiveXP] 5)
	(fade 1)
	(if (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^SEX^)
		(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [%s bashes the panel as hard as she can.  After a moment, it gives.] (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^NAME^)))
		(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [%s bashes the panel as hard as he can.  After a moment, it gives.] (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(if (flag ^LearnedForgeDagger^)
		(saychar 0 300 300 [The panel is now open.])
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [The panel is now open.  %s does not think it's wise to enter.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(cond (ask [<go inside>] [<leave>])
		(	
			(fade 0)
			(
				(if ^PARTY1^
					(moveto ^PARTY1^ 66 891)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY2^
					(moveto ^PARTY2^ 67 891)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY3^
					(moveto ^PARTY3^ 68 891)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY4^
					(moveto ^PARTY4^ 66 890)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY5^
					(moveto ^PARTY5^ 67 890)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY6^
					(moveto ^PARTY6^ 68 890)
					()
				)
			)
			(sflag ^SnuckInJerrock^ 1)
			(sflag ^DisableTravel^ 1)

			(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
			(fade 1)

			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!AlreadyBrokeIn!
	(if (flag ^LearnedForgeDagger^)
		(saychar 0 300 300 [The panel is open.])
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [The panel is now open.  %s does not think it's wise to enter.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(cond (ask [<go inside>] [<leave>])
		(	
			(fade 0)
			(
				(if ^PARTY1^
					(moveto ^PARTY1^ 66 891)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY2^
					(moveto ^PARTY2^ 67 891)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY3^
					(moveto ^PARTY3^ 68 891)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY4^
					(moveto ^PARTY4^ 66 890)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY5^
					(moveto ^PARTY5^ 67 890)
					()
				)
				(if ^PARTY6^
					(moveto ^PARTY6^ 68 890)
					()
				)
			)
			(sflag ^SnuckInJerrock^ 1)
			(sflag ^DisableTravel^ 1)

			(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
			(fade 1)

			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

);BreakBoard;


;EVENT 203;
;TreeClimb;

(#TreeClimb#
(!begin!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 14)
		()
		()
		(goto [SawLimb])
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!SawLimb!
	(if (or (flag ^CarynRebellion^) (flag ^KilledZeph^))
		(  (saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [%s does not think it would be wise to go back into Jerrock.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(if (flag ^LearnedForgeDagger^)
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [%s notices a branch hanging over the fence.  It might be possible for someone to climb in and throw a rope over.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [%s notices a branch hanging over the fence.  It might be possible for someone to climb in and throw a rope over.  %s does not think it's a good idea to enter Jerrock right now.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)(getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Rope^)
		(cond (ask [<attempt to climb the tree>] [<leave>])
			(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^DEXTERITY^) 21)
				(goto [FailedClimb])
				(goto [FailedClimb])
				(goto [SucceededClimb])
			)
			()
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!FailedClimb!
	(fade 0)
	(fade 1)
	(saychar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) 300 300 (string [%s tries scrambling up the tree, but falls off.] (getchar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^NAME^)))
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!SucceededClimb!
	(fade 0)
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 123 915)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 123 914)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 123 913)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 124 915)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 124 914)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 124 913)
			()
		)
	)
	(callscript [GiveXP] 5)
	(sflag ^SnuckInJerrock^ 1)
	(sflag ^DisableTravel^ 1)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) 300 300 (string [%s nimbly scrambles up the tree, climbs across the branch and jumps over the fence, and throws a rope over the side.  The rest of the party makes their way inside.] (getchar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^NAME^)))

	(push 0)
	(end)
)

			()
			()
			()

		()
	)

	(end)
)

		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s spots a trap.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
	)

		()

			()
			()
			()
			(goto [disarmed trap])
			(goto [disarmed trap])
		)

			()
			()
			()
			(goto [disarmed trap])
			(goto [disarmed trap])
		)

			(cond (rand 5)
				()
				()
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])

				()
				()

				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])

					(goto [disarmed trap])
				)
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])

;EVENT 212;

(#CorinsBody#
(!begin!
	(saychar 0 300 300 [There's a ring still attached to the remains of the body.])
	(cond (ask [<take the ring>] [<leave the ring alone>])
		(give ^PARTY^ ^Corin's Ring^)
		(place ^Corin's Ring^ 188 186)
	)
	(push 1)
	(end)
)

);CorinsBody;


;EVENT 213;
;GramBust;

(#GramBust#
(!begin!
	(if (and (flag ^GramBust^) (has ^PARTY^ ^Gram's Head^))
		(goto [Daxius])
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Daxius!
	;move daxius in from offscreen;
	(place ^Daxius^ (getx ^LEAD^) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 10))
	(move ^Daxius^ (getx ^LEAD^) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 2))
	(dofor 80
		(
			(update ^Daxius^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(talk ^Daxius^)
	(push 1)
	(end)
)

);GramBust;

;EVENT 215;
;PartyFacesBarrierCouncil;

(#PartyFacesBarrierCouncil#
(!begin!
	
	(fade 0)

	;clearactions;
	(
		(clearactions ^Lena^)
		(clearactions ^Nina^)
		(clearactions ^Talos^)
		(clearactions ^Saspina^)
		(clearactions ^Damrush^)
		(clearactions ^Vortia^)
		(clearactions ^Tia^)
		(clearactions ^Estabon^)
		(clearactions ^Daxius^)
		(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	)	

	;place officials;
	(
		(place ^Lena^ 2963 2672)
		(if (flag ^NinaDead^)
			()
			(place ^Nina^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 4) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 2))
		)
		(place ^Talos^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 2) (gety ^Lena^))
		(place ^Saspina^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 2) (gety ^Lena^))
		(place ^Damrush^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 3) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 2))
	)

	;place guards;
	(
		(place ^Vortia^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 4) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 4))
		(place ^Tia^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 2) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 7))
		(place ^Estabon^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 4) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 4))
		(place ^Daxius^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 2) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 7))
	)

	;place party;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 4))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ (getx ^Lena^) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 4))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 4))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 5))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ (getx ^Lena^) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 5))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 5))
			()
		)
	)
	
	;face party;

	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(face ^PARTY1^ ^S^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(face ^PARTY2^ ^S^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(face ^PARTY3^ ^S^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(face ^PARTY4^ ^S^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(face ^PARTY5^ ^S^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(face ^PARTY6^ ^S^)
			()
		)
	)

	;face guards and officials;
	(
		(face ^Vortia^ ^SW^)
		(face ^Tia^ ^S^)
		(face ^Daxius^ ^S^)
		(face ^Estabon^ ^SE^)
		(face ^Lena^ ^N^)
		(face ^Talos^ ^N^)
		(face ^Saspina^ ^N^)
		(face ^Damrush^ ^NE^)
		(if (flag ^NinaDead^)
			()
			(face ^Nina^ ^NW^)
		)
	)

	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Lena^)
			(update ^Vortia^)
			(update ^Tia^)
			(update ^Daxius^)
			(update ^Estabon^)
			(update ^Saspina^)
			(update ^Talos^)
			(update ^Damrush^)
			(if (flag ^NinaDead^)
				()
				(update ^Nina^)
			)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)
	(cameralookat ^Lena^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You are brought before the Barrier's high council to stand trial.])


	;check to see if caught with airon;	
	(if (flag ^AironSentHome^)
		(  (saydescchar ^Lena^ 300 300 [Lena seems to recognize you.  She leans forward to get a closer look.])
			(saychar ^Lena^ 300 300 [You!  I know you!  You're the ones who tried to kidnap my son, aren't you?  I suppose you kidnapping murderers thought you would fit in just fine in the Barrier, eh?  Well today you will learn just how wrong you are.  Guards.  Take them to the gallows immediately.])
		)
		(if (inparty ^Airon^)
			(  (saydescchar ^Lena^ 300 300 [Lena turns and points a finger to Airon.])
				(saychar ^Lena^ 300 300 [And you young man, you get yourself back home right now!  You don't even want to imagine what's going to happen to you when I get home!])
				(saychar ^Airon^ 300 300 [Yes Ma'am.])
				(saychar ^Lena^ 300 300 [Take the other ruffians to the gallows immediately.])
			)
			(saychar ^Lena^ 300 300 [I suppose you murdering would be taken lightly here in the Barrier, eh?  Well today you will learn just how wrong you are.  Guards.  Take them to the gallows immediately.])
		)
	)
	
	;move guards;
	(
		(move ^Vortia^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 4) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 9))
		(move ^Tia^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 2) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 13))
		(move ^Estabon^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 4) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 9))
		(move ^Daxius^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 2) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 13))
	)

	;move party;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(move ^PARTY1^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 9))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(move ^PARTY2^ (getx ^Lena^) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 9))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(move ^PARTY3^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 9))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(move ^PARTY4^ (add (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 10))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(move ^PARTY5^ (getx ^Lena^) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 10))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(move ^PARTY6^ (sub (getx ^Lena^) 1) (sub (gety ^Lena^) 10))
			()
		)
	)

	(dofor 25
		(
			(update ^Vortia^)
			(update ^Tia^)
			(update ^Daxius^)
			(update ^Estabon^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
			(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	
	;game over;
	(fade 0)
	(endgame [You and your companions are hanged until dead.])
	(end)
)

;EVENT 216;
;GuardFight;

(#GuardFight#
(!begin!
	

	(removejournal 53)
	(removejournal 55)
	(removejournal 56)


	(saydescchar ^Senzo^ 300 300 [Senzo motions to the other guards.])
	(saychar ^Senzo^ 300 300 [C'mon guys.  Time to settle some business with those fancy boys on the southside.])
	(saychar ^Guidian^ 300 300 [Our pleasure boss.])

	;move senzo, guidian and gareth to the door;
	(move ^Senzo^ 3023 2610)
	(move ^Guidian^ 3023 2610)
	(move ^Gareth^ 3023 2610)
	(dofor 50
		(
			(update ^Senzo^)
			(update ^Guidian^)
			(update ^Gareth^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	;set flags;
	(sflag ^GuardsFighting^ 1)
	(sflag ^GuardsFought^ 1)

	(fade 0)
	(setchar ^Senzo^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Senzo^)
	(removechar ^Senzo^)
	(setchar ^Guidian^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Guidian^)
	(removechar ^Guidian^)
	(setchar ^Gareth^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Gareth^)
	(removechar ^Gareth^)
	(setchar ^Estabon^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Estabon^)
	(removechar ^Estabon^)
	(setchar ^Tia^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Tia^)
	(removechar ^Tia^)
	(fade 1)

	(end)
)

;EVENT 217;
;SenzoFightsMarta;

(#SenzoFightsMarta#
(!begin!

	;clear actions before moving;
	(
		(clearactions ^Senzo^)
		(clearactions ^Guidian^)
		(clearactions ^Gareth^)	
	)

	;move senzo, guidian and gareth to the door;
	(move ^Senzo^ 3023 2610)
	(move ^Guidian^ 3023 2610)
	(move ^Gareth^ 3023 2610)
	(dofor 50
		(
			(update ^Senzo^)
			(update ^Guidian^)
			(update ^Gareth^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(fade 0)
	
	;set flags;
	(sflag ^SolvedGramPath^ 1)
	(sflag ^SolvedMasseque^ 1)
	(flagp ^PartyRep^ 20)

	;clear actions;
	(
		(clearactions ^Marta^)
		(clearactions ^Senzo^)
		(clearactions ^Guidian^)
		(clearactions ^Gareth^)
		(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	)

	;place pre-transformed marta;
	(place ^Marta^ 2959 2685)

	;place masseque in the Kaliyan Inn;
	(schedule ^Masseque^ 2938 2576 2939 2577 0 23 ^W^ 1)

	;place guards;
	(
		(place ^Senzo^ 2963 2686)
		(place ^Guidian^ 2962 2681)
		(place ^Gareth^ 2961 2682)
	)
	
	;place party members;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ (add (getx ^Marta^) 6) (gety ^Marta^))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ (add (getx ^Marta^) 6) (sub (gety ^Marta^) 1))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ (add (getx ^Marta^) 6) (sub (gety ^Marta^) 2))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ (add (getx ^Marta^) 7) (gety ^Marta^))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ (add (getx ^Marta^) 7) (sub (gety ^Marta^) 1))
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ (add (getx ^Marta^) 7) (sub (gety ^Marta^) 2))
			()
		)
	)

	;face guards and marta;
	(
		(face ^Senzo^ ^W^)
		(face ^Guidian^ ^SW^)
		(face ^Gareth^ ^SW^)
		(face ^Marta^ ^E^)
	)

	;face party;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(face ^PARTY1^ ^W^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(face ^PARTY2^ ^W^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(face ^PARTY3^ ^SW^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(face ^PARTY4^ ^W^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(face ^PARTY5^ ^SW^)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(face ^PARTY6^ ^SW^)
			()
		)
	)

	(dofor 15
		(
			(update ^Marta^)
			(update ^Senzo^)
			(update ^Guidian^)
			(update ^Gareth^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)

	(cameralookat ^Marta^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar ^Senzo^ 300 300 [Hello ma'am.  I'm here from the northside barracks.  We've just spoken with a man named Gram, and though I'm sure it's nothing, we'd like to take you to the station just to ask you a few questions.])
	(saydescchar ^Marta^ 300 300 [Marta grins.])
	(saychar ^Marta^ 300 300 [I'm afraid I can't let you do that dear.])
	(saychar ^Gareth^ 300 300 [Let me handle this boss.  Miss, I'm afraid you're going to have to come with us...])

	(move ^Gareth^ (add (getx ^Marta^) 1) (sub (gety ^Marta^) 1))
	(face ^Marta^ ^NE^)
	(dofor 40
		(
			(update ^Gareth^)
			(update ^Marta^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(animate ^Marta^ 7)
	(dofor 20
		(
			(update ^Marta^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Gareth^ 107 ^Marta^)
	(dofor 30
		(
			(update ^Gareth^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(playsound 21)

	(saychar ^Guidian^ 300 300 [What the flame did she do to Gareth?!?])
	(saychar ^Marta^ 300 300 [Come and find out for yourself darling.])
	(saychar 0 300 300 [No sooner has Marta spoken than she begins to transform.])

	(fade 0)
	
	(place ^Transformed Marta^ (getx ^Marta^) (gety ^Marta^))
	
	(setchar ^Transformed Marta^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Transformed Marta^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	
	(removechar ^Marta^)
	(removechar ^Gareth^)
	(face ^Transformed Marta^ ^E^)
	(dofor 10
		(update ^Transformed Marta^)
	)
	(fade 1)


	(
		(setchar ^Senzo^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		(setchar ^Guidian^ ^AICODE^ 1)
	)
	
	(startcombat)

	(end)
)

);SenzoFightsMarta;

	(improveskill (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^)
	(if (equals (getbest ^NATURE^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ -1 -1 [You are familiar with Eian's pet and believe that the animal could be sedated if its belly were properly rubbed.])
		(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) -1 -1 (string [%s, who is familiar with Eian's pet, believes that the animal could be sedated if its belly were properly rubbed.] (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(cond (ask [<attempt to rub fuzzy's belly>] [<fight>])
		(goto [RubFuzzy])
		(goto [KillFuzzy])
	)
)
(!RubFuzzy!
	(
		(fade 0)
		(schedule ^Fuzzy^ 2990 2611 2991 2612 0 23 ^S^ 1)
		(fade 1)
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^DEXTERITY^) ^DEXTERITY^) 14)
			(goto [PokeFuzzy])
			(goto [PokeFuzzy])
			(goto [PassedDex])

	)
)
(!PassedDex!
	(

		;move eian to fuzzy;

			(

				(drawscreen)

		(callscript [GiveXP] 2)

		(if (equals (getchar (getbest ^GIFTS OF GODDESS^) ^GIFTS OF GODDESS^) 0)
			(end)
			(if (flag ^GoddessAngered^)
				(end)
				(  (saychar ^The Goddess^ 300 300 [You have shown great kindess.  Please accept this gift as thanks.])

	(end)
)

		(placeclear ^Wild Penniger^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5))
		(placeclear ^Wild Penniger^ (getx ^LEAD^) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 9))
		(placeclear ^Wild Penniger^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5))

		(setchar ^Borus^ ^AICODE^ 1)
