		;equip objects;
		(equip ^Trovis^ ^Full Ironwood Plate^)
		(equip ^Trovis^ ^Broadsword^)
	)
	
	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar 0 300 300 [The hunting expedition is hardly under way before you and your compatriots realize that you've been surrounded])
	(saychar ^Borus^ 300 300 [What the?!?  Looks like they were waiting for us!])
	(addeventendcombat 238)
)

;EVENT 238;
;HuntedWithBorus;

(#HuntedWithBorus#
(!begin!

	(fade 0)
	;place borus, trovis, and the party back in Trovis's shoup;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 2949 2627)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 2950 2628)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 2951 2629)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 2948 2628)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 2949 2629)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 2950 2630)
			()
		)
		(place ^Borus^ 2954 2627)
		(place ^Trovis^ 2953 2624)
	)

	;face people;
	(face ^Borus^ ^W^)
	(face ^Trovis^ ^SW^)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^NE^)

	(dofor 10
		(
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
			(update ^Borus^)
			(update ^Trovis^)
		)
	)

	;clear locations;
	(setchar ^Borus^ ^CHEST^ 0)
	(setchar ^Borus^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^Borus^ ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Borus^)

	;equip objects;
	(equip ^Borus^ ^Green Quilted Shirt^)

	;clear locations;
	(setchar ^Trovis^ ^CHEST^ 0)
	(setchar ^Trovis^ ^RIGHTHAND^ 0)
	(setchar ^Trovis^ ^LEFTHAND^ 0)
	(equip ^Trovis^)

	;equip objects;
	(equip ^Trovis^ ^Banded Leather Shirt^)

	(fade 1)


   (sflag ^HuntedWithBorus^ 1)
	(saychar ^Borus^ 300 300 [Don't think we owe you anything just cause you helped us out with those Pennigers.  We would of been just fine on our own.])
	(saychar ^Trovis^ 300 300 [Just the same, I'd like to offer you something on the house.  What would you like?])

	(cond (ask [A sword] [A dagger] [An axe] [A blunt weapon] [A polearm] [A missile weapon] [Throwing weapons] [Armor])
		;sword;(give ^PARTY^ ^Shortsword^)
		;dagger;(give ^PARTY^ ^Parrying Dagger^)
		;axe;(give ^PARTY^ ^Ironwood Battleaxe^)
		;blunt;(give ^PARTY^ ^Military Pick^)
		;polearm;(give ^PARTY^ ^Military Fork^)
		;missile;(give ^PARTY^ ^Longbow^)
		;throwing;(give ^PARTY^ ^Vangesh H'kar^ 40)
		;armor;(give ^PARTY^ ^Ironwood Breastplate^)
	)
	
	(saychar ^Trovis^ 300 300 [There you are.  Don't tell anyone I gave it to you or everyone will come around looking for handouts.])

	(push 1)
	(end)
)

);HuntedWithBorus;

		)
	)

	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Daphne^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)


	(fade 1)

	(saydescchar ^Daphne^ 300 300 [Daphne smiles playfully.])
	(saychar ^Daphne^ 300 300 [I'm going to put on something special, just for you.  Don't you go anywhere, but maybe your friends could wait outside?  I'll be back in a sec...])

	(fade 0)
	(clearactions ^Daphne^)
	(removechar ^Daphne^)
	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)
	
	(goto [limitedtime])
)
(!limitedtime!
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^SPEED^) 19)
	   (goto [BELOW20])
	   (goto [BELOW20])
	   (goto [VERYFAST])
	)
)
(!BELOW20!
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^SPEED^) 14)
	   (goto [BELOW15])
	   (goto [BELOW15])
	   (goto [FAST])
	)
)
(!BELOW15!
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^SPEED^) 11)
	   (goto [BELOW12])
	   (goto [BELOW12])
	   (goto [NORMAL])
	)
)
(!BELOW12!
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^SPEED^) 7)
	   (goto [VERYSLOW])
	   (goto [VERYSLOW])
	   (goto [SLOW])
	)
)
(!VERYFAST!
	(cond (comp (flag ^FindCharm^) 5)
		(cond (ask [<look in the dresser cabinet>] [<look under the bed>] [<look in the end table drawer>] [<look in the vase>] [<look on the bookcase>] [<look on the dining table>])
	 		(goto [<look in the dresser cabinet>])
			(goto [<look under the bed>])

			)
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

;EVENT 249;
;FollowZito;

(#FollowZito#
(!begin!

	;check to make sure he's back at the spot;
	(cond (comp (gettotaltime) (add (flag ^ZitoTimeCheck^) 30))
		(  (push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
		()
	)
	
	;check to see if zito's dead;
	(if (flag ^RoloZitoDead^)
		(  (push 1)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)

	;check to see what time it is;
	(cond (comp (gethour) 3)
	   (goto [Night])
	   ()
	   ()
	)
	(cond (comp (gethour) 20)
	   ()
	   ()
	   (goto [Night])
	)

	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Night!
	(if (or (flag ^SawGems^) (flag ^TalkedtoDell^))
		()
		(  (push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)
	(saydescchar ^Zito^ 300 300 [Zito looks nervous.  He seems to be heading back to town carrying something.])
	(cond (ask [<follow him>] [<stay here>])
		(goto [FollowZito])
		(  (fade 0)
			;move zito to the kaliyan inn;
			(clearactions ^Zito^)
			(sflag ^ZitoTimeCheck^ (gettotaltime))
			(place ^Zito^ 2922 2576)
			(fade 1)
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)
)
(!FollowZito!
	(if (equals (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 0)
		(  (fade 0)
			(fade 1)
			(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [While sneaking up behind Zito, you step on a dry twig.  The sound causes Zito to turn around.])

			(saychar ^Zito^ 300 300 [What are you, following me?  Mind your business.])

			(fade 0)
			(sflag ^ZitoTimeCheck^ (gettotaltime))

			;move zito to the kaliyan inn;
			(clearactions ^Zito^)
			(place ^Zito^ 2922 2576)
			(fade 1)
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
		(goto [StealthCheck])
	)
)
(!StealthCheck!
	(if (flag ^ZitoTimeCheck^)
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 40)
			(goto [FailedStealth])
			(goto [FailedStealth])
			(goto [PassedStealth])
		)
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 30)
			(goto [FailedStealth])
			(goto [FailedStealth])
			(goto [PassedStealth])
		)
	)
)
(!FailedStealth!
	(move ^Zito^ 2920 2638)

	(dofor 20
		(
			(update ^Zito^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	;move zito to the kaliyan inn;
	(place ^Zito^ 2922 2576)
	(sflag ^ZitoSuspicious^ 1)

	(saychar ^Zito^ 300 300 [Hey!  Who's there?!?])

	(if (equals (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^Zito^ 300 300 [Zito notices you following him and ducks down an alley.  You lose him and return to the party.])
		(saychar ^Zito^ 300 300 (string [Zito notices %s following him and ducks down an alley.  %s loses him and returns to the party.] (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^NAME^) (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!PassedStealth!
	(move ^Zito^ 2920 2638)

	(dofor 20
		(
			(update ^Zito^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(fade 0)
	(clearactions ^Rolo^)
	(clearactions ^Zito^)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	(place ^Rolo^ 2972 2573)
	(place ^Zito^ 2972 2575)
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 2969 2578)

	(face ^Rolo^ ^S^)
	(face ^Zito^ ^N^)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^NE^)

	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Rolo^)
			(update ^Zito^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)

	(cameralookat ^Zito^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You follow Zito to The Lusty Strumpet where he enters the side door and hands a bag to Rolo.])
	
	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(saychar 0 300 300 [Before you can be seen you back to the gates.])

	(fade 0)
	(sflag ^FollowedZito^ 1)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 12)
		(journal 93)
		(journal 93)
		(journal 94)
   )
		
	;place party near where event was seen;
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 2908 2640)

	;face party;
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^E^)
	(fade 1)

	(callscript [GiveXP] 2)
	(push 1)
	(end)
)

);FollowZito;


;EVENT 250;
;SeeGems;

(#SeeGems#
(!begin!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 18)
		()
		()
		(goto [NoticeBag])
	)
	(if (flag ^TalkedtoDell^)
		(goto [NoticeBag])
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!NoticeBag!
	(sflag ^SawGems^ 1)
	(if (equals (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You notice something out of place peeking out of the long grass.])
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [%s notices something out of place peeking out of the long grass.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(cond (ask [<pick it up>] [<leave it alone>])
		(  (saychar 0 300 300 [It appears to be a bag full of uncut gems.])
			(give ^PARTY^ ^Bag of Gems^)
			(removechar ^Zito^)
			(removechar ^Rolo^)
			(sflag ^RoloZitoDead^ 1)
			(push 1)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

);SeeGems;


;EVENT 251;

(#LookinGemBag#

	(saychar 0 300 300 [It's filled with uncut gems.])

	(end)

;EVENT 253;
;DerrackGetsArrested;

(#DerrackGetsArrested#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^RattedDerrack^)
		(goto [Arrest])
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Arrest!
	(fade 0)
	
	(removejournal 12)
	(removejournal 32)
	(removejournal 70)
	(removejournal 71)
	(removejournal 77)
	(removejournal 79)
	(removejournal 848)

	(if (flag ^DerrackReward^)
		(flagm ^PartyRep^ 15)
		(flagm ^PartyRep^ 5)
	)

	;clearactions;
	(clearactions ^Damrush^)
	(clearactions ^Estabon^)
	(clearactions ^Daxius^)

	;place people;
	(place ^Damrush^ 3012 2597)
	(place ^Estabon^ 3011 2598)
	(place ^Daxius^ 3013 2598)

	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 3011 2594)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 3011 2593)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 3011 2592)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 3012 2594)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 3012 2593)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 3012 2592)
			()
		)
	)

	(cameralookat ^Damrush^)
	(fade 1)

	(move ^Damrush^ 3008 2594)
	(move ^Estabon^ 3009 2593)
	(move ^Daxius^ 3009 2595)

	(dofor 80
		(
			(update ^Damrush^)
			(update ^Estabon^)
			(update ^Daxius^)
			(cameralookat ^Damrush^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(saychar ^Derrack^ 300 300 [Damrush, good to see you.  What's this all about?])
	(saychar ^Damrush^ 300 300 [Save it Derrack.  I'm afraid you've caused us one too many embarrassments.])
	(saychar ^Derrack^ 300 300 [What are you talking...])
	(saychar ^Damrush^ 300 300 [You've visited the Lusty one too many times.  You're making us all look bad.  If you can't figure out on your own what it means to be a Guardian, then it's time you hung up your axe.])
	(saychar ^Damrush^ 300 300 [Your father was an honorable man Derrack.  He would be very disappointed in your actions.  You will come with us until I figure out what to do with you.])
	(if (flag ^DerrackReward^)
		(  (saychar ^Derrack^ 300 300 [Very well.  I will come peacefully.  But I know who did this and they will pay in blood.])
			(saychar ^Damrush^ 300 300 [You will do nothing of the sort!  Come along.])
		)
		(saychar ^Derrack^ 300 300 [Very well.  I will come peacefully.])
	)

	;clearactions;
	(clearactions ^Damrush^)
	(clearactions ^Estabon^)
	(clearactions ^Daxius^)

	(move ^Derrack^ 3012 2597)
	(move ^Damrush^ 3012 2597)
	(move ^Estabon^ 3012 2597)
	(move ^Daxius^ 3012 2597)

	(dofor 50
		(
			(update ^Derrack^)
			(update ^Damrush^)
			(update ^Estabon^)
			(update ^Daxius^)
			(cameralookat ^Derrack^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)

	(fade 0)
	;clearactions;
	(clearactions ^Derrack^)
	(clearactions ^Damrush^)
	(clearactions ^Estabon^)
	(clearactions ^Daxius^)
	(clearactions ^Guidian^)
	(clearactions ^Gareth^)
	(clearactions ^Senzo^)
	
	(journal 85)
	(sflag ^DerrackArrested^ 1)

	(removechar ^Derrack^)
	(removechar ^Daxius^)
	(removechar ^Damrush^)

	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	(fade 1)

	(push 1)
	(end)
)

);DerrackGetsArrested;



;EVENT 254;
;DerracksRevenge;

(#DerracksRevenge#
(!begin!
	(if (and (flag ^DerrackArrested^) (flag ^DerrackReward^))
		(goto [DerrackFight])
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!DerrackFight!

	(if (flag ^DerrackAmbushedParty^)
		( (push 1)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	
	(sflag ^DerrackAmbushedParty^ 1)
	
	;playsound for axe;

	(damage ^LEAD^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) (add (rand 3) 2)) ^Derrack^)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	;playsound 3;
	(dofor 13
		(
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(saychar 0 300 300 [Before you have time to react, a small axe hits you from seemingly out of nowhere.])
	
	(fade 0)

	;clearactions;
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	;place Derrack;
	(place ^Derrack^ 2944 2601)
	
	;face people;
	(face ^Derrack^ ^NW^)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^SE^)
	
	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Derrack^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)

	;re equip derrack;
	(if (flag ^GotDerrackAxe^)
		(equip ^Derrack^ ^Battleaxe of the Flame^)
		()
	)

	(cameralookat ^Derrack^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar ^Derrack^ 300 300 [Didn't expect to see me again, did you?  You sniveling little rat.])

	(setchar ^Derrack^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar ^Derrack^ ^AICODE^ 1)

	(push 2)
	(end)

)

);DerracksRevenge;

	(saychar ^Airon^ 100 100 [Fine, I'll go.  You don't have to bore me to death.])
	(saychar 0 300 300 [Without so much as a goodbye Airon shuffles back to his room.])

	(removenpc ^Airon^)

			(update ^Airon^)
			(drawscreen)
		)

;EVENT 263;

(#NoticeLallia#
(!begin!

	;check time;
	(cond (comp (gethour) 5)
		(goto [night])
		(goto [night])
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (gethour) 19)
		()
		(goto [night])
		(goto [night])
	)
	
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!night!

	(if (flag ^KnowMaleficium^)
		()
		(  (push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 12)
		(  (push 0)
			(end)
		)
		(  (push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)

	(if (equals (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^LEAD^)

		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You notice a woman at the bar who is wearing an enormous amount of gaudy Vangesh jewelry, and very expensive clothes.])
		(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [%s notices a woman at the bar who is wearing an enormous amount of gaudy Vangesh jewelry, and very expensive clothes.] (getchar (getbest ^Intelligence^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(sflag ^NoticedLallia^ 1)
	(callscript [GiveXP] 1)
	(push 1)
	(end)

)
);NoticeLallia;

;EVENT 265;
;MaleficiumHideout;
;DRAFT DONE;
;TESTED;
;PUSH DONE;
;Quest: Char I- Maleficium
What: Party finds the Maleficium HQ
Called by: Minefield in the sewers
Desc.: Should just be a pop up that says they found the Maleficium HQ as listed on the sewer map.;

(#MaleficiumHideout#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^MaleficiumDestroyed^)
		(	(push 2)
			(end)
		)
		()
	);if;
	(if (flag ^FoundMaleficiumHQ^)
		(if (flag ^KnowMaleficium^)
			(describe [You have found the Maleficium headquarters.])
			(describe [You have found the smugglers headquarters.])
		)				
		(	(sflag ^KnowMaleficiumSewers^ 1)
			(sflag ^FoundMaleficiumHQ^ 1)
			(if (flag ^KnowMaleficium^)
				(	(saychar 0 300 300 [You have found the Maleficium headquarters.])
					(sflag ^KnowMaleficiumSewers^ 1)
					(journal 1011)
					(callscript [GiveXP] 1)
					;(addlocation [Maleficium HQ] X Y);
				)
				(	(saychar 0 300 300 [You've stumbled into what looks like the headquarters for a smuggling operation.])
					(sflag ^KnowMaleficiumSewers^ 1)
					(journal 1011)
					(callscript [GiveXP] 1)
					;(addlocation [Maleficium HQ] X Y);
				)
			)
			(push 0)
			(end)	
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
);MaleficiumHideout;


;EVENT 266;
;LearnFlashofFlame;

(#LearnFlashofFlame#
(!begin!
	(if (equals (getchar (getbest ^Power of Flame^) ^Power of Flame^) 0)
		(saychar 0 300 300 [You try to use the game but nothing happens.])
		(goto [Spell])
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!Spell!

	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	

	;ADD SFX TO SPELLS;

	;player learns flash of flame;
	;flash of flame's spellid is 5;
	;fx for all people with power of flame;

	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(if (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^POWER OF FLAME^)
				(  (saydescchar  ^PARTY1^ 300 300 (string [%s learns the Flash of Flame ritual.] (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^NAME^)))
					(addspell ^PARTY1^ 5)
				)
				()
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(if (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^POWER OF FLAME^)
				(  (saydescchar  ^PARTY2^ 300 300 (string [%s learns the Flash of Flame ritual.] (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^NAME^)))
					(addspell ^PARTY2^ 5)
				)
				()
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(if (getchar ^PARTY3^ ^POWER OF FLAME^)
				(  (saydescchar ^PARTY3^ 300 300 (string [%s learns the Flash of Flame ritual.] (getchar ^PARTY3^ ^NAME^)))
					(addspell ^PARTY3^ 5)
				)
				()
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(if (getchar ^PARTY4^ ^POWER OF FLAME^)
				(  (saydescchar ^PARTY4^ 300 300 (string [%s learns the Flash of Flame ritual.] (getchar ^PARTY4^ ^NAME^)))
					(addspell ^PARTY4^ 5)
				)
				()
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(if (getchar ^PARTY5^ ^POWER OF FLAME^)
				(  (saydescchar ^PARTY5^ 300 300 (string [%s learns the Flash of Flame ritual.] (getchar ^PARTY5^ ^NAME^)))
					(addspell ^PARTY5^ 5)
				)
				()
			)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(if (getchar ^PARTY6^ ^POWER OF FLAME^)
				(  (saydescchar ^PARTY6^ 300 300 (string [%s learns the Flash of Flame ritual.] (getchar ^PARTY6^ ^NAME^)))
					(addspell ^PARTY6^ 5)
				)
				()
			)
			()
		)
	)

	(push 0)
	(end)
);begin;

);LearnFlashofFlame;


;EVENT 267;
;MaleficiumDestroyed;
;PUSH DONE;
;Quest: Char I-Maleficium
What: Party kills all of the Maleficum leaders.
Called by: Killing Maleficum leaders
Desc.: Similar to the Lupin Den in Kellen, there shoul be a flag or something that keeps track of when all the Maleficium leaders are killed.;

(#MaleficiumDestroyed#
(!begin!
	(flagp ^DeadMaleficium^ 1)
	;if all are dead set the MaleficiumDestroyed flag to true;
	(if (comp (flag ^DeadMaleficium^) 7)
		(
			(saychar 0 300 300 [You have killed the Maleficium leadership.])
			(sflag ^MaleficiumDestroyed^ 1)
			(callscript [GiveXP] 6)
			(flagp ^PartyRep^ 30)
		)
		()
	)
	(end)
)
);MaleficiumDestroyed;


;EVENT 268;
;CouncilVote;

(#CouncilVote#
(!begin!

	(fade 0)

	;reset flags;
	(sflag ^CharVotes^ 0)
	(sflag ^FlamVotes^ 0)
	(sflag ^LeitVotes^ 0)
	(sflag ^GotLeitRod^ 0)
	(sflag ^GotCharRod^ 0)
	(sflag ^GotTempleRod^ 0)
	(sflag ^GotFlamRod^ 0)

	;clearactions;
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(clearactions ^Nacippe^)
	(clearactions ^Lucien^)
	(clearactions ^Fastoflex^)
	(clearactions ^Priestess^)
	(clearactions ^Otarian^)

	;place people;
	(place ^Nacippe^ 1816 65)
	(place ^Lucien^ 1821 61)
	(place ^Fastoflex^ 1818 59)
	(place ^Priestess^ 1821 65)
	(place ^Otarian^ 1816 61)
	(place ^Actaia^ 1818 46)

	;place party;
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(moveto ^PARTY1^ 1817 67)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY2^
			(moveto ^PARTY2^ 1818 67)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY3^
			(moveto ^PARTY3^ 1819 67)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY4^
			(moveto ^PARTY4^ 1817 68)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY5^
			(moveto ^PARTY5^ 1818 68)
			()
		)
		(if ^PARTY6^
			(moveto ^PARTY6^ 1819 68)
			()
		)
	)

	;face people;
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^N^)
	(face ^Nacippe^ ^NE^)
	(face ^Lucien^ ^W^)
	(face ^Fastoflex^ ^S^)
	(face ^Priestess^ ^NW^)
	(face ^Otarian^ ^E^)

	(dofor 20
		(
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
			(update ^Nacippe^)
			(update ^Lucien^)
			(update ^Fastoflex^)
			(update ^Priestess^)
			(update ^Otarian^)
		)
	)

	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
	(fade 1)

	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [In accordance with our sacred laws a vote has been called.  I shall call on each House in turn, as we have done from the ancient times.])
	(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [The Keeper opens a large tome and prepares to record the votes.])

	;char vote;
	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [How does House Char vote?])
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Char Voting Rod^)
		(	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [You present the House Char voting rod.])

			(cond (ask [House Char votes for House Char] [House Char votes for House Flam] [House Char votes for House Leit])
				( 	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Char has voted for itself.])

					(cameralookat ^Lucien^)
					(saydescchar ^Lucien^ 300 300 [Lucien nods his head in agreement.])
					(flagp ^CharVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(  (cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Char has voted for House Flam.])

					(cameralookat ^Lucien^)
					(saydescchar ^Lucien^ 300 300 [Frowning, Lucien slowly shakes his head.])
					(flagp ^FlamVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(  (cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Char has voted for House Leit.])

					(cameralookat ^Lucien^)
					(saydescchar ^Lucien^ 300 300 [Frowning, Lucien slowly shakes his head.])
					(flagp ^LeitVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
			)
		)
		(  (cameralookat ^Lucien^)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [Lucien of House Char gets to his feet unsteadily, and clears his voice.])
			(saychar ^Lucien^ 300 300 [House Char votes for itself, House Char.])
			(flagp ^CharVotes^ 1)

			(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
			(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
		)		
	)

	;flam vote;
	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [How does House Flam vote?])
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Flam Voting Rod^)
		(	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [You present the House Flam voting rod.])
			(cond (ask [House Flam votes for House Char] [House Flam votes for House Flam] [House Flam votes for House Leit])
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Flam has voted for House Char.])

					(cameralookat ^Nacippe^)
					(saydescchar ^Nacippe^ 300 300 [Nacippe snarls at you.])
					(flagp ^CharVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(  (cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Flam has voted for itself.])

					(cameralookat ^Nacippe^)
					(saydescchar ^Nacippe^ 300 300 [Nacippe smiles at you.])
					(flagp ^FlamVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Flam has voted for House Leit.])

					(cameralookat ^Nacippe^)
					(saydescchar ^Nacippe^ 300 300 [Nacippe snarls at you.])
					(flagp ^LeitVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
			)
		)
		(  (cameralookat ^Nacippe^)
			(saydescchar ^Nacippe^ 300 300 [Nacippe of House Flam stands, and speaks clearly, despite her advancing age.])
			(saychar ^Nacippe^ 300 300 [The House of Flam votes for the House of Flam.])
			(flagp ^FlamVotes^ 1)
			
			(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
			(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
		)		
	)

	;leit vote;
	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [How does House Leit vote?])
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Leit Voting Rod^)
		(	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [You present the House Leit voting rod.])

			(cond (ask [House Leit votes for House Char] [House Leit votes for House Flam] [House Leit votes for House Leit])
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Leit has voted for House Char.])

					(cameralookat ^Otarian^)
					(saydescchar ^Otarian^ 300 300 [Otarian looks at you in astonishment.])
					(flagp ^CharVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Leit has voted for House Flam.])

					(cameralookat ^Otarian^)
					(saydescchar ^Otarian^ 300 300 [Otarian looks at you in astonishment.])
					(flagp ^FlamVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that House Leit has voted for itself.])
					
					(cameralookat ^Otarian^)
					(saydescchar ^Otarian^ 300 300 [Otarian looks at you with a look of pleasant surprise.])
					(flagp ^LeitVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
			)
		)
		(	(cameralookat ^Otarian^)
			(saydescchar ^Otarian^ 300 300 [Otarian of House Flam stands, his voice cracking with age.])
			(saychar ^Otarian^ 300 300 [I vote for House Leit.])
			(flagp ^LeitVotes^ 1)

			(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
			(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
		)		
	)

	;leit priestess;
	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [How does the Temple of the Flame vote?])
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Temple Voting Rod^)
		(	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [You present the Temple voting rod.])

			(cond (ask [The Temple votes for House Char] [The Temple votes for House Flam] [The Temple votes for House Leit])
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that the Temple has voted for House Char.])
					
					(cameralookat ^Priestess^)
					(saydescchar ^Priestess^ 300 300 [The Priestess's calm demeanor doesn't change as you place the vote.])				
					(flagp ^CharVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that the Temple has voted for House Flam.])
					
					(cameralookat ^Priestess^)
					(saydescchar ^Priestess^ 300 300 [The Priestess's calm demeanor doesn't change as you place the vote.])				
					(flagp ^FlamVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
				(	(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [I record that the Temple has voted for House Leit.])
					
					(cameralookat ^Priestess^)
					(saydescchar ^Priestess^ 300 300 [The Priestess's calm demeanor doesn't change as you place the vote.])				
					(flagp ^LeitVotes^ 1)

					(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
					(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex records the vote in a large tome.])
				)
			)
		)
		(	(cameralookat ^Priestess^)
			(saydescchar ^Priestess^ 300 300 [The Priestess stands, her vestments billowing around her like an aura.])
			(saychar ^Priestess^ 300 300 [The Temple of the Flame abstains.])
			
			(cameralookat ^Fastoflex^)
			(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Fastoflex nods slightly.])
		)
	)
	(goto [Vote Check])
);vote begin;

(!Vote Check!

	;char check;
	(if (comp (flag ^CharVotes^) 3)
		(	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Based on votes cast this day, in accordance with the sacred laws of the Children of the Flame, I declare that House Char wins the position of Council leader, which they will fill for the next two years.  I shall send criers to announce the news to the entire Valley.])
			(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [The Keeper takes your voting rods.])
			(journal 1003)
			(sflag ^CharWinsVote^ 1)
			(goto [Reward])
		)
		();not enough;
	)

	;flame check;
	(if (comp (flag ^FlamVotes^) 3)
		(	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Based on votes cast this day, in accordance with the sacred laws of the Children of the Flame, I declare that House Flam wins the position of Council leader, which they will fill for the next two years.  I shall send criers to announce the news to the entire Valley.])
			(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [The Keeper takes the voting rods.])
			(sflag ^FlamWinsVote^ 1)
			(journal 1004)
			(goto [Reward])
		)
		();not enough;
	)

	;leit check;
	(if (comp (flag ^LeitVotes^) 3)
		(	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300  [Based on votes cast this day, in accordance with the sacred laws of the Children of the Flame, I declare that House Leit wins the position of Council leader, which they will fill for the next two years.  I shall send criers to announce the news to the entire Valley.])
			(saydescchar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [The Keeper takes the voting rods.])
			(sflag ^LeitWinsVote^ 1)
			(journal 1005)
			(goto [Reward])
		)
		()
	)

	(saychar ^Fastoflex^ 300 300 [Again, an election has been called but there are not yet enough votes to win the election.  We shall return again when the next vote is called.])
)
(!Reward!
	
	;place lucien;
	(place ^Lucien^ 32 21 [citadel2])

	;take rods;
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Char Voting Rod^)
		(take ^PARTY^ ^Char Voting Rod^)
		()
	)
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Flam Voting Rod^)
		(take ^PARTY^ ^Flam Voting Rod^)
		()
	)
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Leit Voting Rod^)
		(take ^PARTY^ ^Leit Voting Rod^)
		()
	)
	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Temple Voting Rod^)
		(take ^PARTY^ ^Temple Voting Rod^)
		()
	)

	;xp;
	(callscript [GiveXP] 4)

	;removejournals;
	(removejournal 913)
	(removejournal 914)
	(removejournal 946)
	(removejournal 997)
	(removejournal 1000)
	(removejournal 1001)
	(removejournal 1002)

	;flags;
	(flagp ^PartyRep^ 50)
	(sflag ^CouncilResolved^ 1)

	(end)
);Vote Check;

);CouncilVote;


;EVENT 269;
;ValonianDuel;
;Quest: Char III-Rogue General
What: Duel between one party member and Valonian of Char
Called by: Valonian
Desc.: The party challenges (or is challenged by) Valonian. The player chooses one character to duel Valonian, who is armed with exceptionally high quality armor and weapons, including a steel longsword.;

(#ValonianDuel#
(!begin!
	(fade 0)
	(sflag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^ (getchar ^LEAD^ ^SWORD^))
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^DAGGER^)(flag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^))
		()
		()
		(sflag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^ (getchar ^LEAD^ ^DAGGER^))
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^AXE^)(flag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^))
		()
		()
		(sflag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^ (getchar ^LEAD^ ^AXE^))
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^BLUNT^)(flag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^))
		()
		()
		(sflag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^ (getchar ^LEAD^ ^BLUNT^))
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^LEAD^ ^POLEARM^)(flag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^))
		()
		()
		(sflag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^ (getchar ^LEAD^ ^POLEARM^))
	)

	;clear actions;
	(clearactions ^Valonian^)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	;place people;
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ 72 34)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ 73 34)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ 74 34)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ 75 34)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ 76 34)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ 77 34)
		()
	)
	(moveto ^LEAD^ 74 26)
	(moveto ^Valonian^ 75 26)

	;face people;
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(face ^PARTY1^ ^N^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(face ^PARTY2^ ^N^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(face ^PARTY3^ ^N^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(face ^PARTY4^ ^N^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(face ^PARTY5^ ^N^)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(face ^PARTY6^ ^N^)
		()
	)

	(face ^Valonian^ ^W^)
	(face ^LEAD^ ^E^)

	(dofor 10
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)


	(camerazoom 10)
	;camera rotate;

	(clearactions ^Valonian^)
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	(cameralookat ^Valonian^)
	(fade 1)
	(saychar ^Valonian^ 100 100 [Shall we?])
	
		(animate ^LEAD^ 9)
			(dofor 10
				(
					(update ^LEAD^)
					(drawscreen)
				)
			)	
			(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
			(dofor 10
				(
					(update ^Valonian^)
					(drawscreen)
				)
			)
		
			(animate ^LEAD^ 9)
			(dofor 10
				(
					(update ^LEAD^)
					(drawscreen)
				)
			)

	(animate ^LEAD^ 9)
	(animate ^Valonian^ 17)
	(dofor 15
		(
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Valonian^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^LEAD^)

	(cond (comp	(flag ^ValDuelWeaponSkill^) 45)
		(goto [LoseDuel])
		(goto [LoseDuel])
		(goto [WinDuel])
	)
);begin;
(!LoseDuel!
	;Valonian hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^LEAD^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^Valonian^)

	;Valonian hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^LEAD^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^Valonian^)

	;Valonian hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^LEAD^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^Valonian^)

	;Valonian hits and damages player;
	(clearactions ^Valonian^)
	(clearactions ^LEAD^)
	(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^LEAD^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^Valonian^)

	(saychar ^Valonian^ 100 100 [I'm done playing, whelp.  It's time for this game to end.])

	(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	(damage ^LEAD^ (sub (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 1) ^Valonian^)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(sflag ^ValonianWins^ 1)
	(journal 961)
	(saychar ^Valonian^ 100 100 [Pathetic.])
);LoseDuel;
(!WinDuel!
	;Valonian hits and damages player;
	(sflag ^BeatValonianDuel^ 1)
	(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^LEAD^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^Valonian^)

	;Valonian hits and damages player;
	(animate ^Valonian^ 9)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^LEAD^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^Valonian^)

	;LEAD hits and damages Valonian;
	(animate ^LEAD^ 9)
	(animate ^Valonian^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Valonian^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^LEAD^)

	;Valonian hits and damages player;
	(clearactions ^Valonian^)
	(clearactions ^LEAD^)
	(animate ^LEAD^ 9)
	(animate ^Valonian^ 17)
	(dofor 11
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(damage ^Valonian^ (div (getchar ^LEAD^ ^HITPOINTS^) 8) ^LEAD^)

	(animate ^LEAD^ 9)
	(animate ^Valonian^ 17)
	(damage ^Valonian^ (add (getchar ^Valonian^ ^HITPOINTS^) 25) ^LEAD^)
	(dofor 25
		(
			(update ^Valonian^)
			(update ^LEAD^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	(journal 960)
	(fade 0)
	(removechar ^Valonian^)
	(fade 1)
	(saychar 0 100 100 [The assembled Guardians stare in disbelief at their fallen leader.  Then without a word they one by one gather their possessions and leave the encampment.])
	(callscript [GiveXP] 6)
	(flagp ^PartyRep^ 50)
	(fade 0)
	(if (getat 48 29)
		(removechar (getat 48 29))
		()
	)
	(if (getat 50 44)
		(removechar (getat 50 44))
		()
	)
	(if (getat 73 17)
		(removechar (getat 73 17))
		()
	)
	(if (getat 79 33)
		(removechar (getat 79 33))
		()
	)
	(if (getat 67 34)
		(removechar (getat 67 34))
		()
	)
	(if (getat 90 50)
		(removechar (getat 90 50))
		()
	)
	(if (getat 85 51)
		(removechar (getat 85 51))
		()
	)
	(fade 1)
)
)

;EVENT 273;
;EncampmentFight;

			(setchar ^Otelius^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
	)

		()
		()
		(	(setchar ^Destius^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Destius^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^Plaenius^ ^HITPOINTS^) 0)
		()
		()
		(	(setchar ^Plaenius^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Plaenius^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
	)
	(if (getat 48 29)
		(	(setchar (getat 48 29) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar (getat 48 29) ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
		()
	)
	(if (getat 50 44)
		(	(setchar (getat 50 44) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar (getat 50 44) ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
		()
	)
	(if (getat 73 17)
		(	(setchar (getat 73 17) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar (getat 73 17) ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
		()
	)
	(if (getat 79 33)
		(	(setchar (getat 79 33) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar (getat 79 33) ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
		()
	)
	(if (getat 67 34)
		(	(setchar (getat 67 34) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar (getat 67 34) ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
		()
	)
	(if (getat 90 50)
		(	(setchar (getat 90 50) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar (getat 90 50) ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
		()

;EVENT 274;
;NacippeBox;
;DRAFT DONE;
;NOT TESTED;
;PUSH DONE;
;Quest: Flam II-Courier
What: Party open's Nacippe's box
Called by: Item ^Nacippe's Box^
Desc.: Should take a very high Tinker check to open the box. Inside should be an item of extreme value which the party should be tempted to keep. Valuable jewelry?;

(#NacippeBox#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^TookNB^)
		(saychar 0 300 300 [This box is empty.])
		(if (flag ^PickedNB^)
			(	(saychar 0 300 300 [Inside the box there is a circlet of crystal.])
				(cond (ask [<take the loot>] [<leave the loot>])
					( 	(give ^LEAD^ ^Crystal Torque^)
						(flagm ^PartyRep^ 30)
						(sflag ^TookNB^ 1)
					)
					()
				)
			)
			(  (saychar 0 300 300 [The box is locked.])
				(if (equals (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 0)
					()
					(cond (ask [<attempt to pick the lock>] [<leave the lock alone>])
						(  (fade 0)
							(fade 1)
							(advancetime 5)
							(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 19)
								(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
									(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You fail to pick the lock.])
									(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s fails to pick the lock.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
								)
								(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
									(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You fail to pick the lock.])
									(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s fails to pick the lock.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
								)
								(  (sflag ^PickedNB^ 1)
									(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
										(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You successfully pick the lock.  Inside the box there is circlet of crystal.])
										(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s successfully picks the lock.  Inside the box there is circlet of crystal.] (getchar 	(getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
									)
									(cond (ask [<take the loot>] [<leave the loot>])
										(  (give ^LEAD^ ^Crystal Torque^)
											(flagm ^PartyRep^ 30)
											(sflag ^TookNB^ 1)
										)
										()
									);cond;
								);sflag;
							);cond;
						);fade;
						()
					);cond;
				);if;
			);saychar;
		);if;
	);if;
	(push 1)
	(end)
);begin;

);NacippeBox;


;EVENT 275;

(#DwellerSubdual#

	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Subdual Staff^)
		(goto [subdueBeast])
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!subdueBeast!
	(cameralookat (getat 52 94))

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You spot the beast.])
	(saychar 0 300 300 [Carefully you sneak upon it place the hook around its neck.])
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 48 98)
	(saychar 0 300 300 [Using the subdual staff you are able to pacify the creature and bring it to Rosa.])

	(sflag ^SubduedBeast^ 1)	
	(callscript [GiveXP] 2)
	
	(fade 0)
	;place NPCs;
	;removechar the dweller has been eliminated for work-around;
	
	
	(  
		(goarea [valley] 2009 119)
		(place ^Rosa^ 2009 119)

		(place ^Dweller Leader^ 2007 121)
		(face (getat 2007 121) ^E^)
		(setchar (getat 2007 121) ^BATTLESIDE^ 0)
		(setchar (getat 2007 121) ^AICODE^ 0)
	)

	;place party;
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ (getx ^Rosa^) (add (gety ^Rosa^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ (sub (getx ^Rosa^) 1) (add (gety ^Rosa^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ (add (getx ^Rosa^) 1) (add (gety ^Rosa^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ (sub (getx ^Rosa^) 2) (add (gety ^Rosa^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ (add (getx ^Rosa^) 2) (sub (gety ^Rosa^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^	
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ (sub (getx ^Rosa^) 3) (add (gety ^Rosa^) 4))
		()
	)
	
	(callscript [FaceLocation] (getx ^Rosa^) (gety ^Rosa^))	
	(face ^Rosa^ ^S^)
	(dofor 15
		(	(update ^Rosa^)
			(update (getat 2007 121))
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
		)
	)


	(camerazoom 5)	
	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	
	(fade 1)
	
	(talk ^Rosa^)
	
	(push 1)
	(end)

)
);DwellerSubdual;

(#FollowSaures#

		()

			(end)
		)
	)

			(push 1)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	
	;check time;

		(goto [night])
		(goto [night])
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (gethour) 23)
		()
		(goto [night])
		(goto [night])
	)


	(saychar (getbest ^Stealth^) -1 -1  [The party could probably conceal themselves here and observe the blackmailer coming and going.])
	(push 0)
	(end)

);begin;
(!night!
	;check to see if stealth his high enough;
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 15)
		(  (goto [FailedSauresStealth])
		)
		(  (goto [FailedSauresStealth])
		)
		()
	)

		(update ^Saures^)
	)

	(dofor 20
		(update ^Saures^)
	)
	
	(cond (ask [<follow>] [<let them go>])
		()
		( (push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)

	(if (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 20)
		(
		 (saydescchar 0 -1 -1 [You follow the figure to a building in the western citadel.])
		 (moveto ^PARTY^ 1819 190)
		)
		(
			(saydescchar 0 -1 -1 [The figure glances back.])
			(saydescchar ^Saures^ -1 -1 [...])
			(saydescchar 0 -1 -1 [She dashes off and quickly leaves you behind.])
		)
	)
	
	(fade 0)
	
	(moveto ^Saures^ 1821 198)
	
	(clearactions ^Saures^)
	(setchar ^Saures^ ^HEAD^ 0)
	(equip ^Saures^)
	
	(fade 1)
	
	(push 2) ; stop whatever she was doing;
	(end)
);night;
(!FailedSauresStealth!

	(saydescchar (getworst ^Stealth^) -1 -1 (string [%s sneezes loudly!] (getchar (getworst ^Stealth^) ^Name^)))
	(saydescchar ^LEAD^ -1 -1 [Someone runs away, you scramble to see them, but it is too late; they are gone.])

						(goto [read])

)

	(push 0)
	(end)
)

						(end)

					()

			()
			()

					(cond (ask [<attack the hunter from surprise>] [<talk to the hunter>] [<leave the hunter alone>])
						(	(damage ^Numitor^ 10 ^Numitor^)
							(setchar ^Numitor^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

							(end)

							(push 1)
							(end)

						()
					)
				)

					(cond (ask [<attack the hunter from surprise>] [<talk to the hunter>] [<leave the hunter alone>])
						(	(damage ^Numitor^ 10 ^Numitor^)
							(setchar ^Numitor^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)

	(goarea [valley] (getx ^Priestess^) (gety ^Priestess^))

	(
		(clearactions ^Priestess^)
		(clearactions ^Vectrix^)
		(clearactions ^Fastoflex^)
		(clearactions ^Actaia^)
		(clearactions ^Covinian^)
		(clearactions ^Otionia^)
		(clearactions ^Sipo^)
	)


	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])

	;place NPCs;
	
	(  
		(place ^Priestess^ 1818 60)
		(face ^Priestess^ ^S^)	
		
		(place ^Vectrix^ (getx ^Priestess^) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 4))
		(face ^Vectrix^ ^N^)

		(place ^Fastoflex^ (add (getx ^Priestess^) 2) (gety ^Priestess^))
		(face ^Fastoflex^ ^S^)
		(place ^Actaia^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 2) (gety ^Priestess^))
		(face ^Actaia^ ^S^)
		(place ^Covinian^ (sub (getx ^Vectrix^) 2) (gety ^Vectrix^))
		(face ^Covinian^ ^N^)
		(place ^Otionia^ (getx ^Vectrix^) (add (gety ^Vectrix^) 2))
		(face ^Otionia^ ^N^)
		(place ^Sipo^ (add (getx ^Vectrix^) 2) (gety ^Vectrix^))
		(face ^Sipo^ ^N^)
	)

	;place party;
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(moveto ^PARTY1^ (getx ^Priestess^) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 8))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(moveto ^PARTY2^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 1) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 8))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(moveto ^PARTY3^ (add (getx ^Priestess^) 1) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 8))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(moveto ^PARTY4^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 2) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 8))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(moveto ^PARTY5^ (add (getx ^Priestess^) 2) (sub (gety ^Priestess^) 8))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^	
		(moveto ^PARTY6^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 3) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 8))
		()
	)
	

	(dofor 50
		(	(update ^Priestess^)
			(update ^Vectrix^)
			(update ^Fastoflex^)
			(update ^Actaia^)
			(update ^Olvar^)
			(update ^Covinian^)
			(update ^Otionia^)
			(update ^Sipo^)
		)
	)

	(callscript [FaceLocation] (getx ^Priestess^) (gety ^Priestess^))	

	(camerazoom 10)	
	(cameralookat ^Vectrix^)
	
	(fade 1)
	
	;trial;
	
	(saychar 0 300 300 [Vectrix is brought before the Priestess who listens to the testimony.])
	(saychar ^Priestess^ 50 100 [Enough.  I cannot permit these crimes against the Flame and humanity.  You will be hung at dawn.  Take him away.])			
	(saychar ^Vectrix^ 100 200 [Please...])
	(saychar ^Priestess^ 50 100 [Guardians, escort him out and prepare him for the gallows.])
	(sflag ^VectrixExecuted^ 1)
	
	;move guardians and vectrix towards off screen then fade;

	(
		(move ^Covinian^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 8) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 6))
		(move ^Vectrix^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 9) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 6))
		(move ^Otionia^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 10) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 6))
		(move ^Sipo^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 11) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 6))
	)

	(dofor 80
		(
			(update ^Covinian^)
			(update ^Vectrix^)
			(update ^Otionia^)
			(update ^Sipo^)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	
	(fade 0)
	
	(removechar ^Covinian^)
	(removechar ^Vectrix^)
	(removechar ^Otionia^)
	(removechar ^Sipo^)
	
	(fade 1)
	
	(saychar ^Priestess^ 300 300 [Please, come forward.])
	
	;move party forward towards priestess;
	(callscript [ClearActionsParty])
	
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(move ^PARTY1^ (getx ^Priestess^) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(move ^PARTY2^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 1) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(move ^PARTY3^ (add (getx ^Priestess^) 1) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY4^
		(move ^PARTY4^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 2) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY5^
		(move ^PARTY5^ (add (getx ^Priestess^) 2) (add (gety ^Priestess^) 4))
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY6^
		(move ^PARTY6^ (sub (getx ^Priestess^) 3) (sub (gety ^Priestess^) 4))
		()
	)
	
	(dofor 30
		(
			(callscript [UpdateParty])
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	
	(talk ^Priestess^)


)
);VectrixTrial;

;EVENT 291;
;CrematoriumGrate;
;DRAFT DONE;
;NOT TESTED;
;PUSH DONE;
;Quest: Priestess II-Body Snatcher
Called by: Minefield
Desc.: Party enters Rekindling Chamber of the Crematorium, and a sucessful Int check will reveal that the drain grate has been moved. A successful STR check will allow the party to remove the grate, revealing a ladder into the sewers.;

(#CrematoriumGrate#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^MersiusPermission^)
		()
		(  (saychar ^Mersius^ 300 300 [Get away from there.])
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)

	(saychar 0 300 300 [The drain grate is closed.])
	(if (flag ^GrateOpened^)
		(cond (ask [<climb down>] [<leave>])
			(goto [Climb Down])
			()
		)
		(cond (ask [<attempt to open>] [<leave>])
			(goto [<attempt to open>])
			()
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

(!<attempt to open>!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^STRENGTH^) 13)
		(
			(if (equals (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^LEAD^)
				(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [The grate is too small for more than one person to help lift it.  Being the strongest you bend over and attempts to lift the grating, but fail.])
				(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [The grate is too small for more than one person to help lift it.  %s, the strongest, bends over and attempts to lift the grating, but fails.] (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^Name^ )))
			)
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
		()
	)
	(if (equals (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [The grate is too small for more than one person to help lift it.  Being the strongest you bend over and attempt to lift it.  With some effort you are able to move it, exposing a drain which leads, if the smell is any indication, to the sewers.])
		(saychar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) 300 300 (string [The grate is too small for more than one person to help lift it.  %s, the strongest, bends over and attempts to lift the grating, but fails. With some effort the grate moves, exposing a drain which leads, if the smell is any indication, to the sewers.] (getchar (getbest ^STRENGTH^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(sflag ^GrateOpened^1)
	(callscript [GiveXP] 1)
	(cond (ask [<climb down>] [<leave>])
		(goto [Climb Down])
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
);attempt to open;

(!Climb Down!
	(saychar 0 300 300 [You climb down into the stench.])
	(fade 0)
	(goarea [citadelsewers] 26 198)
	(callscript [FaceDirection] ^N^)
	
	(dofor 15
		(callscript [UpdateParty])
	)
	
	(cameralookat ^LEAD^)
	
	(fade 1)
	
	(push 0)
	(end)

);Climb Down;

);CrematoriumGrate;

;EVENT 293;
;VectrixJournal;
;DRAFT DONE;
;NOT TESTED;
;PUSH DONE;
;Quest: Priestess II-Body Snatcher
What: If some with Medical uses the journals they learn more.
Called by: Item
Desc.: If you don't kill Vectrix an agree to let him continue hi work he will give you his journals, which comprise his collected knowledge of anatomy and diseases. Someone with Medical of at least 5 can use the journals to increase their Medical skill (should be at trainer of at least 3 skill, maybe 2).;

(#VectrixJournal#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^ReadVectrix^)
		(saychar 0 300 300 [Your party has already learned all it can from this tome.])
		(	(saychar 0 300 300 [Who will read the journal?])
			(callscript [ChoosePartyMember])
			(cond (comp (getchar (lookstack) ^MEDICAL^) 19)
				(saychar (lookstack) 300 300 (string[The technical details of the journal are beyond %s's ability to understand them.] (getchar (lookstack) ^NAME^)))
				(saychar (lookstack) 300 300 (string[The technical details of the journal are beyond %s's ability to understand them.] (getchar (lookstack) ^NAME^)))
				(	(saychar (lookstack) 300 300 (string[%s dives into Vectrix's journal, and devours the contents.  Although admitting that Vectrix's experimental methods may have been misguided the results are astounding.  %s is confident that the information will be very useful in the future.] (getchar (lookstack) ^NAME^) (getchar (lookstack) ^NAME^)))
					(describe (string [%s's Medical skill increases.] (getchar (lookstack) ^NAME^)))
					(callscript [ImproveSkillValue] [getskills.txt] (pop) ^MEDICAL^ 3)
					(sflag ^ReadVectrix^ 1)
				)
			)
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
);VectrixJournal;

;EVENT 295;
;IulianaVault;
;DONE;
;TESTED;
;PUSH DONE;
;Quest: Music Box
What: Party tries to break into a locked vault.
Called by: Location-locked vault
Desc.: The vault is both locked and alarmed. INT check to notice Alarm, Tinker to deactivate alarm, tinker to open vault (deactivate alarm should be the harderest tinker check int his qust).;

(#IulianaVault#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^TookIV^)
		(	(saychar 0 300 300 [The cabinet is empty.])
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (gethour) 9)
		(goto [night])
		()
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (gethour) 22)
		()
		()
		(goto [night])
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar ^Iuliana^ ^HITPOINTS^) 0)
		(goto [night])
		(goto [night])
		(	(saychar ^Iuliana^ 300 300 [What are you doing?  Get away from there!])
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)
);begin;
(!night!
	(if (flag ^PickedIulianaVault^)
		(	(saychar 0 300 300[Inside the cabinet is a bag of drachs and an intricately carved ironwood box.])
			(cond (ask [<take loot>] [<leave loot>])
				(	(saychar 0 300 300 [You pocket the drachs and wooden box, and appear to be completely in the clear.])
					(sflag ^TookIV^ 1)
					(give ^LEAD^ ^Music Box^)
					(flagp ^PartyDrachs^ 100)
					(callscript [GiveXP] 2)
					(push 0)
					(end)
				)
				(	(push 0)
					(end)
				)
			)		
		)
		()
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 15)
		(goto [Miss alarm])
		(goto [Miss alarm])
		(	(if (equals (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^LEAD^)
				(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [As you approach the cabinet you notice that is alarmed.])
				(saychar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) 300 300 (string [As you approach the cabinet %s notices that it is alarmed.] (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^Name^ )))
			)
			(cond (ask [<attempt to disable alarm>] [<ignore alarm and pick lock>] [<leave>])
				(goto [Disable])
				(goto [Ignore])
				(	(push 0)
					(end)
				)
			)
		)
	)
);night;

(!Miss alarm!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 14)
		(goto [Tinker fail])
		(goto [Tinker fail])
		(goto [Tinker succeed])
	)
);Miss alarm;

(!Tinker fail!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You settle down to crack the lock, and it becomes obvious that the lock is too complex.  Just when you are about to give up, you hear a clicking sound, and a loud bell begins to ring.])
		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s settles down to crack the lock, and it becomes obvious that the lock is too complex.  Just when you are about to give up, you hear a clicking sound and a loud bell begins to ring.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(ask [<more>])
	(goto [Alarm])
);Tinker fail;

(!Tinker succeed!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You settle down to crack the lock, but when you open the cabinet an alarm goes off.  Inside the cabinet you see a bag of drachs and a small intricately carved ironwood box.])
		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s settles down to crack the lock, but when the cabinet opens an alarm goes off.  Inside the cabinet you see a bag of drachs and a small intricately carved ironwood box.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(cond (ask [<take loot>] [<escape>])
		(	(sflag ^TookIV^ 1)
			(give ^LEAD^ ^Music Box^)
			(flagp ^PartyDrachs^ 100)
			(callscript [GiveXP] 2)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [Just as you finish pocketing the drachs and wooden box, you are surrounded by Guardians.])
			(sflag ^AlarmTripped^ 1)
			(sflag ^IulianaVexed^ 1)
			(ask [<more>])
			(event 296)
		)
		(goto [Escape])
	)
);Tinker succeed;

(!Disable!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 19)
		(goto [Disable fail])
		(goto [Disable fail])
		(goto [Disable succeed])
	)
);Disable;

(!Ignore!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 14)
		(goto [Ignore fail])
		(goto [Ignore fail])
		(goto [Ignore succeed])
	)
);Ignore;

(!Disable fail!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(	(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You settle down to try to disable the alarm, but instead you set it off.])
			(ask [<more>])	
			(goto [Alarm])
		)
		(	(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s settles down to try to disable the alarm, but instead sets it off.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^Name^ )))
			(ask [<more>])	
			(goto [Alarm])
		)
	)
);Disable fail;

(!Disable succeed!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(	(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You settle down to try to disable the alarm, and you sigh with relief when you succeed.])
			(cond (ask [<attempt to pick lock>] [<leave>])
				(goto [Disable picked])
				(	(push 0)
					(end)
				)
			)
		)
		(	(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s settles down to try to disable the alarm, and succeeds.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^Name^ )))
			(cond (ask [<attempt to pick lock>] [<leave>])
				(goto [Disable picked])
				(	(push 0)
					(end)
				)
			)
		)
	)
);Disable succeed;

(!Disable picked!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(	(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [Compared to the alarm the lock is easy to pick.  Inside the cabinet you see a bag of drachs and a small intricately carved ironwood box.])
			()
		)
		(	(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s finds the cabinet lock to be easy to pick compared to disabling the alarm.  Inside the cabinet you see a bag of drachs and a small intricately carved ironwood box.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^Name^ )))
		)
	)
	(cond (ask [<take loot>] [<leave loot>])
		(	(sflag ^TookIV^ 1)
			(give ^LEAD^ ^Music Box^)
			(callscript [GiveXP] 2)
			(flagp ^PartyDrachs^ 100)
			(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You pocket the drachs and wooden box, and appear to be completely in the clear.])
		)
		(	(sflag ^PickedIulianaVault^ 1)
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
	)
);Disable picked;

(!Ignore fail!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You settle down to crack the lock, and it becomes obvious that the lock is too complex.  Just when you are about to give up, you hear a clicking sound, and the alarm you ignored starts to ring.])
		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s settles down to crack the lock, and it becomes obvious that the lock is too complex.  Just when you are about to give up, there is a clicking sound, and the previously ignored alarm starts to ring.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(ask [<more>])	
	(goto [Alarm])
);Ignore fail;

(!Ignore succeed!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You settle down to crack the lock, but when you open the cabinet the alarm you ignored goes off.  Inside the cabinet you see a bag of drachs and a small intricately carved ironwood box.])
		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s settles down to crack the lock, but when the cabinet opens the previously ignored alarm goes off.  Inside the cabinet you see a bag of drachs and a small intricately carved ironwood box.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^Name^ )))
	)
	(cond (ask [<take loot>] [<escape>])
		(	(sflag ^TookIV^ 1)
			(give ^LEAD^ ^Music Box^)
			(callscript [GiveXP] 2)
			(flagp ^PartyDrachs^ 100)
			(sflag ^AlarmTripped^ 1)
			(sflag ^IulianaVexed^ 1)
			(saychar 0 300 300 [Just as you finish pocketing the drachs and wooden box, you are surrounded by Guardians.])
			(ask [<more>])
			(event 296)
		)
		(goto [Escape])
	)
);Ignore succeed;
(!Escape!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 35)
		(goto [Alarm])
		(goto [Alarm])
		(goto [Escape Success])
	)
)

(!Escape Success!
	(fade 0)
	(moveto ^PARTY^ 1875 118)
	(fade 1)
	(saychar 0 300 300 [You manage to escape before the Guardians arrive.])
	(end)
)

(!Alarm!
	(push 0)
	(saychar 0 300 300 [You suddenly find yourself surrounded by Guardians.])
	(ask [<more>])
	(sflag ^AlarmTripped^ 1)
	(sflag ^IulianaVexed^ 1)
	(event 296)
);Alarm;

);IulianaVault;


;EVENT 296;
;TurnusArrest;
;Quest: Catch a Thief;
;Turnus shows up with guardians and confronts party;
;What: Party is arrested for breaking into Iuliana's house;
;if alarm tripped ;
;teleport guardians in;

(#TurnusArrest#
(!begin!
	;place guards;
	(if (flag ^TurnusAttacked^)
		(goto [GenericGuards])
		(goto [TurnusGuards])
	)
)
(!GenericGuards!
	(fade 0)
	(flagm ^PartyRep^ 200)
	(
		(if ^PARTY1^
			(clearactions ^PARTY1^)
			()

;EVENT 298;
;CourierStealh;
;DRAFT DONE;
;NOT TESTED;
;PUSH DONE;
;Quest: Illegal Courier
What:  Party appraoches Hiera's ambush
Called by: Minefield 
Desc.: As the party approaches the drop location they have a chance of seeing the waiting Guardians before they get busted.;


(#CourierStealh#
(!begin!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 20)
		(sflag ^HieraSuspicious^ 1)
		(sflag ^HieraSuspicious^ 1)
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 13)
			(	(saydesc [In the shadows you see a group of figures, who appear to be watching the drop location.])
				(sflag ^HieraSuspicious^ 1)
				(sflag ^SawHiera^ 1)
			)
			(	(saydesc [In the shadows you see a group of figures, who appear to be watching the drop location.])
				(sflag ^HieraSuspicious^ 1)
				(sflag ^SawHiera^ 1)
			)
			(	(saydesc (string [In the shadows you see a group of figures, who appear to be watching the drop location.  %s thinks they're Guardians.] (getchar (getbest ^Intelligence^) ^NAME^)))		
				(sflag ^SawHiera^ 1)
				(sflag ^HieraSuspicious^ 1)
				(callscript [GiveXP] 1)
			)
		)
	)
	(push 1)
	(end)
)
);CourierStealh;

(#LibraryTrapOne#

			()
			()
			(goto [saw trap])
			(goto [saw trap])
		)

			()
			()
			()
			(goto [saw trap])
			(goto [saw trap])
		)
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 20)
			(cond (rand 5)
				()
				()
				(goto [saw trap])
				(goto [saw trap])
				(goto [saw trap])
			)
			(cond (rand 5)
				()
				()
				(goto [saw trap])
				(goto [saw trap])
				(goto [saw trap])

				(cond (rand 5)
					()
					(goto [saw trap])
					(goto [saw trap])
					(goto [saw trap])
					(goto [saw trap])
				)
				(goto [saw trap])
				(goto [saw trap])
			)
		)
	)

		(saychar 0 300 300 [Before you can react there is a loud pop.  You are damaged by flying shrapnel.])
		(saychar 0 300 300 [Before you can react arrows are flying around you at all angles.])
		(saychar 0 300 300 [Before you can react a hidden snare snaps up from the earth.])
	)
	(goto [trap detonated])
)
(!trap detonated!
	(if ^PARTY1^
		(cond (rand 3)
			(damage ^PARTY1^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 3))
			(damage ^PARTY1^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 4))
			(damage ^PARTY1^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 5))
		)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY2^
		(cond (rand 3)
			(damage ^PARTY2^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 3))
			(damage ^PARTY2^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 4))
			(damage ^PARTY2^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 5))
		)
		()
	)
	(if ^PARTY3^
		(cond (rand 3)
			(damage ^PARTY3^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 3))
			(damage ^PARTY3^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 4))

			(damage ^PARTY6^ (div (getchar ^PARTY1^ ^MAXHITPOINTS^) 5))

	)

	(end)
);trap detonated;
(!saw trap!
	(if (equals (getbest ^TINKER^) ^LEAD^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You spot a trap.])
		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s spots a trap.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
	)
	(cond (ask [<attempt to disarm the trap>] [<leave>])
		()
		(
			(if (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^)
				()
				(
					(if (equals (getworst ^STEALTH^) ^LEAD^)
						(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You accidentally trigger the trap.])
						(saychar (getworst ^STEALTH^) 300 300 (string [%s accidentally triggers the trap.] (getchar (getworst ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))
					)
					(goto [trap detonated])
				)
			)
		)
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 10)
		(cond (rand 5)
			()
			()
			()
			(goto [disarmed trap])
			(goto [disarmed trap])
		)

			()
			()
			()
			(goto [disarmed trap])
			(goto [disarmed trap])
		)
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 20)
			(cond (rand 5)
				()
				()
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])
			)
			(cond (rand 5)
				()
				()
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])
			)
			(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^TINKER^) 30)
				(cond (rand 5)
					()
					(goto [disarmed trap])
					(goto [disarmed trap])
					(goto [disarmed trap])
					(goto [disarmed trap])
				)
				(goto [disarmed trap])
				(goto [disarmed trap])
			)
		)

			(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You accidentally hit the wrong switch.])
			(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You accidentally pull the wrong lever.])
			(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You accidentally press the wrong button.])

		(saychar (getbest ^TINKER^) 300 300 (string [%s successfully disarms the trap.] (getchar (getbest ^TINKER^) ^NAME^)))

;EVENT 321;
(#VirmornAttacks#
(!begin!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 45)
		()
		()
		(  (push 1)
			(end)
		)
	)

	(if (flag ^VirmornReward^)
		()
		(if (or (has ^PARTY^ ^Zorella's Ring^) (has ^PARTY^ ^Seppus' Helm^) (has ^PARTY^ ^Ignatian Battle Gauntlets^))
			(goto [Attack])
			()
		)
	)

	;If the party comes up from the archives with more than one of the relics from the locked room below.;

	(if (has ^PARTY^ ^Zorella's Ring^)
		(if (or (has ^PARTY^ ^Seppus' Helm^) (has ^PARTY^ ^Ignatian Battle Gauntlets^))
			(goto [Attack])
			()
		)
		(if (and (has ^PARTY^ ^Seppus' Helm^) (has ^PARTY^ ^Ignatian Battle Gauntlets^))
			(goto [Attack])
			()
		)
	)
	(push 1)
	(end)
)
(!Attack!
	(saychar ^Virmorn^ 50 100 [The Flame does not smile upon thieves.])
	
	;all people set against the player;

	(if (inparty ^Bashnakh^)
		()
		(  (setchar ^Bashnakh^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Bashnakh^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)
	)

	(

		(  (setchar ^Virmorn^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Virmorn^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Bashnakh^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Bashnakh^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Zand^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Zand^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Rangard^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Rangard^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Shambul^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Shambul^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Khal^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Khal^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Charl^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Charl^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Oghakh^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Oghakh^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Lugram^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Lugram^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Sharag^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Sharag^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

		(  (setchar ^Bazir^ ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
			(setchar ^Bazir^ ^AICODE^ 1)
		)

	)
	(push 1)
	(end)

   (drawscreen)
  )

 	)
 )

(dofor 15
    ( 
        (updateall)
        (drawscreen)
    )
)

 
 (clearactions ^Khal^)
 (removechar ^Khal^)
 ;Sharag unleashes a fireball upon Khal, killing him;
 
 (if (equals (getchar (getbest ^POWER OF FLAME^) ^POWER OF FLAME^) 0)
  (journal 646)
  (
 
   (journal 644)

     ()
    )
    ()

     (  (addspell ^PARTY2^ 1)
      (describe (string [Fireball has been added to %s's spellbook.] (getchar ^PARTY2^ ^NAME^)))

     ()
    )

   )

;EVENT 328;
;JerahdRoot;

(#JerahdRoot#
(!begin!
	(pop) ;remove user of root from stack;
	(if (flag ^SharagSentGoddess^)
		(goto [SharagSentGoddess])
		()
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)
(!SharagSentGoddess!
	(if (flag ^GoddessWeak^)
		(	(saychar [There is no response.])
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	(if (flag ^GoddessVexedPerm^)
		(	(saychar [There is no response.  It looks as though the Goddess truly has forsaken you.])
			(push 0)
			(end)
		)
		()
	)
	
	(if (flag ^GoddessAngered^)
		(	(saychar ^The Goddess^ 50 100 [You have done wrong.  Until you have atoned for your crimes you shall go without my aid.])
			(sflag ^GoddessDidntCall^ 1)
		)
		(  (callscript [GoddessAppear] (add (getx ^LEAD^) 4) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 4))
			(cameralookat ^The Goddess^)
			(if (flag ^GoddessCall^)
				(saychar ^The Goddess^ 50 100 [I have called for you.  Why has the Uilir River not brought you to me?  Hurry, time is short.])	
				(	(saychar ^The Goddess^ 50 100 [I must see you.  You must come to my isle.  The Uilir River can bring you.  Hurry.  The darkness grows.])
					(sflag ^GoddessCall^ 1)
					(journal 675)
				)
			)

			(fade 0)
			(place ^The Goddess^ 1614 1197)
			(fade 1)
			
		)
	)
	(push 0)
	(end)
)

);JerahdRoot;

;EVENT 332;
;Valley areas;

(#ValleyEvents#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^KellenDead^)
		(  (event 371)
			(end)
		)

		(cond (comp (gettotaltime) 20000)
			(goto [easy])
			(goto [easy])
			(goto [hard])
		)

	)
);begin;
(!easy!
	(cond (rand 11)
	;1;(push 338);Hopper;
	;2;(push 338);Hopper;
	;3;(push 339);Chicken;
	;4;(push 337);Lupin;
	;5;(push 340);Topi;
	;6;(push 341);FriendlyPersonEncounter;
	;7;(push 341);FriendlyPersonEncounter;
	;8;(push 342);Hunters;
	;9;(push 343);Bandit;
	;10;(push 372);Battlefield;
	;11;(push 373);Body;
	)
	(goto [end])
);easy;
(!hard!
	(cond (rand 11)
	;1;(push 337);Lupin;
	;2;(push 339);Chicken;
	;3;(push 340);Topi;
	;4;(push 341);FriendlyPersonEncounter;
	;5;(push 342);Hunters;
	;6;(push 343);Bandit;
	;7;(push 343);Bandit;
	;8;(push 372);Battlefield;
	;9;(push 373);Body;
	;10;(if (getchar ^Malornian^ ^HITPOINTS^)
			(push 344);Malornian;
			(push 337);Lupin;
		)
	;11;(if (getchar ^Malornian^ ^HITPOINTS^)
			(push 344);Malornian;
			(push 339);Chicken;
		)
	)
	(goto [end])
);hard;
(!end!
	;check to see which event was last;
	(if (or (equals (flag ^LatestEvent^) (lookstack)) (equals (flag ^LatestLatestEvent^) (lookstack)))
		(
			(pop)
			(goto [begin])
		)
		(event (pop))
	)
)

);ValleyEvents;


;EVENT 333;
;mountains areas;

(#HillEvents#
(!begin!
	(if (flag ^KellenDead^)
		(  (event 371)
			(end)
		)

		(cond (comp (gettotaltime) 20000)
			(goto [easy])
			(goto [easy])
			(goto [hard])
		)
	)
);begin;
(!easy!
	(cond (rand 8)
	;1;(push 345);Creeper;
	;2;(push 346);Mantid;
	;3;(push 358);Dweller;
	;4;(push 341);FriendlyPersonEncounter;
	;5;(push 342);Hunters;
	;6;(push 343);Bandit;
	;7;(push 372);Battlefield;
	;8;(push 373);Body;
	)
	(goto [end])
);easy;
(!hard!
	(cond (rand 8)
	;1;(push 345);Creeper;
	;2;(push 346);Mantid;
	;3;(push 358);Dweller;
	;4;(push 341);FriendlyPersonEncounter;
	;5;(push 372);Battlefield;
	;6;(push 373);Body;
	;7;(if (getchar ^Malornian^ ^HITPOINTS^)
			(push 344);Malornian;
			(push 345);Creeper;
		)
	;8;(if (getchar ^Malornian^ ^HITPOINTS^)
			(push 344);Malornian;
			(push 346);Mantid;
		)
	)
	(goto [end])

);hard;
(!end!
	;check to see which event was last;
	(if (or (equals (flag ^LatestEvent^) (lookstack)) (equals (flag ^LatestLatestEvent^) (lookstack)))
		(
			(pop)
			(goto [begin])
		)
		(event (pop))
	)
)

);HillEvents;

;EVENT 337;
;LupinEncounter;

(#LupinEncounter#
(!begin!
	
	(sflag ^LatestLatestEvent^ (flag ^LatestEvent^))
	(sflag ^LatestEvent^ 337)
	(improveskill (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^)
				
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Nature^) 10)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^) 20)
			(
				(if (equals (getbest ^NATURE^) ^LEAD^)
					(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 [You think you hear something ahead.])
					(if (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^SEX^)
						(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) 300 300 (string [%s thinks she hears something ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
						(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) 300 300 (string [%s thinks he hears something ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
					)
				)
				(goto [options])
			)
			()
			()
		)
	)
	(if (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^SEX^)
		(saychar ^LEAD^ 300 300 (string [%s thinks she hears Lupins ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
		(saychar (getbest ^NATURE^) 300 300 (string [%s thinks he hears Lupins ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
	)
	(goto [options])
)
(!options!
	(cond (ask [<approach>]  [<hide and hope they pass>]  [<flee in the opposite direction>] [<create a diversion and sneak around>])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [hide])
		(goto [flee])
		(goto [diversion])
	)
)
(!diversion!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 15)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (rand 3)
			(goto [placeBaddies])
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [By creating a cunning diversion, you successfully avoid confrontation.])
			)
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [By creating a cunning diversion, you successfully avoid confrontation.])
			)
		)
	)
	(end)
)
(!hide!
	(if (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^)
		()
		(goto [placeBaddies])
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 15)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (rand 3)
			(goto [placeBaddies])
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [The Lupins pass without noticing you.])
			)
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [The Lupins pass without noticing you.])
			)
		)
	)
	(end)
)
(!flee!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getworst ^SPEED^) ^SPEED^) 12)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (rand 3)
			(goto [placeBaddies])
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [You manage to avoid confrontation by running away.])
			)
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [You manage to avoid confrontation by running away.])
			)
		)
	)
	(end)
)
(!placeBaddies!

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You've been attacked by Lupins.])

	;every month add another lupin;
	(dofor (div (gettotaltime) 40320)
		(placeclear ^Lupin^ 
			;x axis;
			(cond (rand 5)
				(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
				(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
				(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
				(add (getx ^LEAD^) 8)
				(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 10)
			)
			;y axis;
			(cond (rand 5)
				(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
				(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
				(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
				(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 8)
				(add (gety ^LEAD^) 10)
			)
		)
	)

	(placeclear ^Lupin^ 
		;x axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
			(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
		;y axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
			(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
	)
)

);LupinEncounter;

;EVENT 339;

(#ChickenEncounter#

		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])

			(
				(if (equals (getbest ^NATURE^) ^LEAD^)

					(if (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^SEX^)

			)
			()
			()
		)

		(saychar 0 300 300 (string [%s thinks she hears Pennigers ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
		(saychar 0 300 300 (string [%s thinks he hears Pennigers ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))

(!flee!

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You've been attacked by Pennigers.])

	;place penniger;
	(placeclear ^Penniger^ 
		;x axis;

			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
			(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)

			;x axis;

				(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
				(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
				(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
			)

			)

;EVENT 340;
;TopiEncounter;

(#TopiEncounter#
(!begin!

	(sflag ^LatestLatestEvent^ (flag ^LatestEvent^))
	(sflag ^LatestEvent^ 340)

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You encounter wild Topi.])

	(sflag ^AddaCreature^ (add (rand 4) 1))

	(dofor (flag ^AddaCreature^)
		(  
			(push (placeclear ^Topi^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) (rand 5)) (add (gety ^LEAD^) (rand 5))))

			(move (lookstack) (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 20) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 20))
			
		)
	)

	(dofor 80
		(
			(updateall)
			(drawscreen)
		)
	)
	
	(dofor (flag ^AddaCreature^)
		(
			(removechar (pop))
		)
	)
)

);TopiEncounter;


;EVENT 341;
;FriendlyPersonEncounter;

(#FriendlyPersonEncounter#
(!begin!

	(sflag ^LatestLatestEvent^ (flag ^LatestEvent^))
	(sflag ^LatestEvent^ 341)

	(fade 0)
	
	(if (inparty ^Tauna^)
		(cond (rand 5)
		;1;(push ^Penthesilia^)
		;2;(push ^Unoro^)
		;3;(push ^Xenoclea^)
		;4;(push ^Aanna^)
		;5;(push ^Venatio^)
		);cond;
		(cond (rand 7)
		;1;(push ^Penthesilia^)
		;2;(push ^Unoro^)
		;3;(push ^Xenoclea^)
		;4;(push ^Aanna^)
		;5;(push ^Venatio^)
		;6;(push ^Tauna^)
		;7;(push ^Tauna^)
		);cond;
	);if;

	(placeclear 
		;person;
		(lookstack)	

		;x axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
			(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
		;y axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
			(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
	)

	(cameralookat (lookstack))
	(fade 1)
	(saydescchar (pop) -1 -1 [You've come across another traveler.])
	(end)
);begin;

);FriendlyPersonEncounter;

;EVENT 343;
;BanditEncounter;

(#BanditEncounter#
(!begin!

	(sflag ^LatestLatestEvent^ (flag ^LatestEvent^))
 	(sflag ^LatestEvent^ 343)

	(cond (rand 4)
		(goto [Ambush])
		(goto [NotAmbushed])
		(goto [NotAmbushed])
		(goto [NotAmbushed])
	)
)
(!Ambush!

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You've been ambushed by bandits!])

	(push (placeclear ^BanditA^ (getx ^LEAD^) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 9)))
	(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
	
	(push (placeclear ^BanditC^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
	(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
	
	(if (or (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 10000))
	(
			(push (placeclear ^BanditA^ (add (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
		)
		()
	)
	
	(if (or (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 15000))
	(
			(push (placeclear ^BanditC^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 5) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 7)))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
		)
		()
	)
	
	(if (or (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 20000))
		(
			(push (placeclear ^BanditA^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 9) (gety ^LEAD^)))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
		)
		()
	)
	
	(if (and (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 30000))
			(
				(push (placeclear ^BanditA^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 7) (add (gety ^LEAD^) 5)))
				(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
			)
			()
	)
	
	(if (and (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 40000))
			(
				(push (placeclear ^BanditA^ (sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6) (sub (gety ^LEAD^) 7)))
				(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
			)
			()
	)
)
(!NotAmbushed!
	(improveskill (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^)
				
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Nature^) 10)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^NATURE^) 20)
			(
				(if (equals (getbest ^NATURE^) ^LEAD^)
					(saychar 0 300 300 [You think you hear something ahead.])
					(if (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^SEX^)
						(saychar 0 300 300 (string [%s thinks she hears something ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
						(saychar 0 300 300 (string [%s thinks he hears something ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
					)
				)
				(goto [options])
			)
			()
			()
		)
	)
	(if (getchar (getbest ^NATURE^) ^SEX^)
		(saychar 0 300 300 (string [%s thinks she hears Bandits ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
		(saychar 0 300 300 (string [%s thinks he hears Bandits ahead.] (getchar (getbest ^Nature^) ^Name^)))
	)
	(goto [options])
)
(!options!
	(cond (ask [<approach>]  [<hide and hope they pass>]  [<flee in the opposite direction>] [<create a diversion and sneak around>])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [hide])
		(goto [flee])
		(goto [diversion])
	)
)
(!diversion!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^INTELLIGENCE^) ^INTELLIGENCE^) 15)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (rand 3)
			(goto [placeBaddies])
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [By creating a cunning diversion, you successfully avoid confrontation.])
			)
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [By creating a cunning diversion, you successfully avoid confrontation.])
			)
		)
	)
	(end)
)
(!hide!
	(if (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^)
		()
		(goto [placeBaddies])
	)
	(cond (comp (getchar (getbest ^STEALTH^) ^STEALTH^) 15)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (rand 3)
			(goto [placeBaddies])
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [The Bandits pass without noticing you.])
			)
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [The Bandits pass without noticing you.])
			)
		)
	)
	(end)
)
(!flee!
	(cond (comp (getchar (getworst ^SPEED^) ^SPEED^) 12)
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(goto [placeBaddies])
		(cond (rand 3)
			(goto [placeBaddies])
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [You manage to avoid confrontation by running away.])
			)
			(  (fade 0)
				(fade 1)
				(saychar 0 300 300 [You manage to avoid confrontation by running away.])
			)
		)
	)
	(end)
)
(!placeBaddies!
	(saychar 0 300 300 [You've been attacked by Bandits.])

	(push (placeclear ^BanditB^ 
		;x axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
			(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
		;y axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
			(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
	))
	(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
	
	(push (placeclear ^BanditC^ 
		;x axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
			(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
		;y axis;
		(cond (rand 3)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
			(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
		)
	))
	(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
	
	(if (or (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 10000))
		(
			(push (placeclear ^BanditD^ 
				;x axis;
				(cond (rand 3)
					(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
					(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
					(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
				)
				;y axis;
				(cond (rand 3)
					(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
					(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
					(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
				)
			))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
		)
		()
	)
	
	(if (or (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 20000))
		(
			(push 
			(placeclear ^BanditD^ 
				;x axis;
				(cond (rand 3)
					(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
					(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
					(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
				)
				;y axis;
				(cond (rand 3)
					(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
					(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
					(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
				)
			))
			(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
		)
		()
	)
	
	(if (and (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 30000))
			(
				(push 
				(placeclear ^BanditD^ 
					;x axis;
					(cond (rand 3)
						(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
						(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
						(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
					)
					;y axis;
					(cond (rand 3)
						(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
						(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
						(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
					)
				))
				(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
			)
			()
	)
	
	(if (and (rand 3) (comp (gettotaltime) 40000))
				(
					(push 
					(placeclear ^BanditD^ 
						;x axis;
						(cond (rand 3)
							(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
							(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
							(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
						)
						;y axis;
						(cond (rand 3)
							(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
							(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
							(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
						)
					))
					(callscript [Randomize Bandit])
				)
				()
	)
	

)

			)
			(  (fade 0)

			)

	)
	(end)
)

	(saychar 0 300 300 [You've been attacked by a thaumaturgist and his golem.])

	(setchar ^Malornian^ ^THROWING^ 20)
	(equip ^Malornian^ ^Throwing Knife^ 20)
	(placeclear ^Malornian^ 
		;x axis;
		(cond (rand 5)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 2)
			(add (getx ^LEAD^) 4)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 6)
			(add (getx ^LEAD^) 8)
			(sub (getx ^LEAD^) 10)
		)
		;y axis;
		(cond (rand 5)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 2)
			(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 4)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 6)
			(sub (gety ^LEAD^) 8)
			(add (gety ^LEAD^) 10)
		)
	)
	(callscript [FindClearSpot] (getx ^LEAD^) (gety ^LEAD^) (sub (getx ^LEAD^) (rand 7)) (add (gety ^LEAD^) (rand 7)) ^Earth Golem^)

	;for earth golem;
	(push (getat (flag ^CX^) (flag ^CY^)))
	(setchar (lookstack) ^BATTLESIDE^ 1)
	(setchar (pop) ^AICODE^ 1)
)
